docs\stream1_transformer_backends.md:124:The native CUTLASS parity path sets `BEAM_STREAM1_SYNTHETIC_STATES=1` so it uses the same deterministic arange-pattern state batch as the Torch/LibTorch benchmark paths.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:8:User direction is explicit: no distillation into MLP and no fallback paths. This work keeps the Stream1 `piece_transformer` backend as a real transformer backend and optimizes it directly.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:12:The current Kaggle p900 `piece_transformer` backend is correct but not yet optimized like the existing MLP backend.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:32:The immediate route is to make this exact transformer backend production-grade:
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:34:1. Add a transformer Stream1 microbenchmark.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:45:A smaller transformer such as 2 layers, `d_model=128..192`, and `ff_dim=512..768` could reduce GEMM/scratch cost while staying a transformer backend. It requires relaxing current exporter/runtime shape checks, which currently reject non-4-layer/non-1024-FF models.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:47:Risk: medium quality loss. Runtime friendliness: medium. This is valid only as an explicit transformer backend variant, not a fallback to MLP.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:57:This is not an MLP fallback. It would be a new explicit piece-token backend with transformer-like token processing but no softmax attention. It is mostly GEMM plus fused bias/activation/residual kernels and removes attention score/probability scratch.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:69:Do current-backend optimization first: benchmark, remove redundant clears, remove global attention scratch, tune transformer-specific batching/concurrency, and then consider smaller transformer or piece-mixer variants. Do not train or wire an MLP distillation path for this track.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:72:After the native CUDA/CUTLASS v19 extended 2xT4 sweep, the best verified native aggregate is `1224735.7` candidates/s, about `0.971x` of the PyTorch `batch_process` reference aggregate. The native experiments remain archived under `docs/archive/stream1_transformer_native/`.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:74:The transformer direction is now three explicit execution backends for the same `piece_transformer` model family: `pytorch`, `libtorch`, and `native_cutlass`. This does not change the manifest model backend, does not distill to MLP, and does not add fallback behavior. Default builds remain Torch-free; the C++ LibTorch benchmark/tool target is enabled only with `BEAM_ENABLE_LIBTORCH_STREAM1=ON`.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:76:Production dispatcher integration remains a separate step for non-native paths because the current dispatcher captures Stream1+Stream2 CUDA Graph templates. LibTorch op dispatch must either be proven graph-capture safe with stable allocations or run through a named non-graph Stream1 execution path. The PyTorch path stays a Python/Kaggle execution backend unless an explicit bridge is designed.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:79:A later Docker Nsight pass compared the explicit C++ LibTorch/cuBLAS path against the native CUTLASS Stream1 transformer path on local RTX 3070. The native CUTLASS backend is already the practical cuBLAS replacement route for production: local b384/c1 reached `871235.6` candidates/s and b512/c2 reached `991974.7`, versus local LibTorch eager `578741`. Nsight Compute 2025.1.1 confirmed both cuBLASLt and CUTLASS sampled GEMMs use HMMA tensor cores.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:81:The current bottleneck is not missing tensor-core use. Native CUTLASS sampled kernels are around `40-43%` SM throughput and `62-70%` DRAM throughput, so the next work should tune transformer-specific GEMM/epilogue/layout/LayerNorm structure. A wider `128x128x32` QKV/FF1 tile regressed versus the current `128x64x32` tile and was reverted. Evidence: `test_results/stream1_transformer_cutlass_vs_libtorch_profile_2026-07-04.md`.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:84:The backend registry is `tools/stream1_transformer_backends.py`; details are in `docs/stream1_transformer_backends.md`.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:86:The three supported backend names are:
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:88:- `pytorch`: Python/Torch execution, including the Kaggle notebook path and exported-weight Torch benchmark.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:89:- `libtorch`: explicit C++ LibTorch execution over exported weights.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:90:- `native_cutlass`: native C++/CUDA/CUTLASS execution through the existing Stream1 transformer runtime.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:95:The three Stream1 `piece_transformer` backends now share a parity launcher contract. PyTorch and LibTorch benchmarks emit `checksum` and `first_score_keys`, and the native CUTLASS transformer microbenchmark emits the same fields while optionally using `BEAM_STREAM1_SYNTHETIC_STATES=1` for deterministic synthetic inputs.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:97:`tools/stream1_transformer_parity.py` is the correctness gate for comparing explicit backends on one synthetic batch. It supports a dry-run mode for clean checkout validation and an execution mode for GPU hosts with exported weights and built backend binaries. This is only a verification layer; production dispatch still selects one backend explicitly and must not silently fall back.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:100:The backend parity contract now includes `score_key_digest`, a deterministic FNV-1a 64-bit digest over all quantized score keys in batch-major, move-major order. The digest is emitted by the PyTorch benchmark, the C++ LibTorch benchmark, and the native CUTLASS transformer microbenchmark. Parity execution should treat digest mismatch as a hard correctness failure; checksum and first-row score keys are secondary diagnostics.

docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:4:Branch: `codex/stream1-piece-transformer`
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:8:User direction is explicit: no distillation into MLP and no fallback paths. This work keeps the Stream1 `piece_transformer` backend as a real transformer backend and optimizes it directly.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:12:The current Kaggle p900 `piece_transformer` backend is correct but not yet optimized like the existing MLP backend.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:22:- `transformer_layers=4`
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:32:The immediate route is to make this exact transformer backend production-grade:
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:34:1. Add a transformer Stream1 microbenchmark.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:35:2. Tune transformer-specific `B_MICRO`, concurrency, ring slots, and shard sizing separately from MLP.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:36:3. Remove redundant transformer scratch clears.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:45:A smaller transformer such as 2 layers, `d_model=128..192`, and `ff_dim=512..768` could reduce GEMM/scratch cost while staying a transformer backend. It requires relaxing current exporter/runtime shape checks, which currently reject non-4-layer/non-1024-FF models.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:47:Risk: medium quality loss. Runtime friendliness: medium. This is valid only as an explicit transformer backend variant, not a fallback to MLP.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:57:This is not an MLP fallback. It would be a new explicit piece-token backend with transformer-like token processing but no softmax attention. It is mostly GEMM plus fused bias/activation/residual kernels and removes attention score/probability scratch.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:63:A transformer-only reranking stage could be considered later, but not as a fallback. It would need explicit Stream1 semantics: score all required candidates or define a formally safe two-stage scoring contract before Stream2/3/4 consume score keys.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:69:Do current-backend optimization first: benchmark, remove redundant clears, remove global attention scratch, tune transformer-specific batching/concurrency, and then consider smaller transformer or piece-mixer variants. Do not train or wire an MLP distillation path for this track.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:72:After the native CUDA/CUTLASS v19 extended 2xT4 sweep, the best verified native aggregate is `1224735.7` candidates/s, about `0.971x` of the PyTorch `batch_process` reference aggregate. The native experiments remain archived under `docs/archive/stream1_transformer_native/`.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:74:The transformer direction is now three explicit execution backends for the same `piece_transformer` model family: `pytorch`, `libtorch`, and `native_cutlass`. This does not change the manifest model backend, does not distill to MLP, and does not add fallback behavior. Default builds remain Torch-free; the C++ LibTorch benchmark/tool target is enabled only with `BEAM_ENABLE_LIBTORCH_STREAM1=ON`.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:76:Production dispatcher integration remains a separate step for non-native paths because the current dispatcher captures Stream1+Stream2 CUDA Graph templates. LibTorch op dispatch must either be proven graph-capture safe with stable allocations or run through a named non-graph Stream1 execution path. The PyTorch path stays a Python/Kaggle execution backend unless an explicit bridge is designed.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:79:A later Docker Nsight pass compared the explicit C++ LibTorch/cuBLAS path against the native CUTLASS Stream1 transformer path on local RTX 3070. The native CUTLASS backend is already the practical cuBLAS replacement route for production: local b384/c1 reached `871235.6` candidates/s and b512/c2 reached `991974.7`, versus local LibTorch eager `578741`. Nsight Compute 2025.1.1 confirmed both cuBLASLt and CUTLASS sampled GEMMs use HMMA tensor cores.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:81:The current bottleneck is not missing tensor-core use. Native CUTLASS sampled kernels are around `40-43%` SM throughput and `62-70%` DRAM throughput, so the next work should tune transformer-specific GEMM/epilogue/layout/LayerNorm structure. A wider `128x128x32` QKV/FF1 tile regressed versus the current `128x64x32` tile and was reverted. Evidence: `test_results/stream1_transformer_cutlass_vs_libtorch_profile_2026-07-04.md`.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:84:The backend registry is `tools/stream1_transformer_backends.py`; details are in `docs/stream1_transformer_backends.md`.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:89:- `libtorch`: explicit C++ LibTorch execution over exported weights.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:90:- `native_cutlass`: native C++/CUDA/CUTLASS execution through the existing Stream1 transformer runtime.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:95:The three Stream1 `piece_transformer` backends now share a parity launcher contract. PyTorch and LibTorch benchmarks emit `checksum` and `first_score_keys`, and the native CUTLASS transformer microbenchmark emits the same fields while optionally using `BEAM_STREAM1_SYNTHETIC_STATES=1` for deterministic synthetic inputs.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:97:`tools/stream1_transformer_parity.py` is the correctness gate for comparing explicit backends on one synthetic batch. It supports a dry-run mode for clean checkout validation and an execution mode for GPU hosts with exported weights and built backend binaries. This is only a verification layer; production dispatch still selects one backend explicitly and must not silently fall back.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:100:The backend parity contract now includes `score_key_digest`, a deterministic FNV-1a 64-bit digest over all quantized score keys in batch-major, move-major order. The digest is emitted by the PyTorch benchmark, the C++ LibTorch benchmark, and the native CUTLASS transformer microbenchmark. Parity execution should treat digest mismatch as a hard correctness failure; checksum and first-row score keys are secondary diagnostics.

docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:8:User direction is explicit: no distillation into MLP and no fallback paths. This work keeps the Stream1 `piece_transformer` backend as a real transformer backend and optimizes it directly.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:12:The current Kaggle p900 `piece_transformer` backend is correct but not yet optimized like the existing MLP backend.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:32:The immediate route is to make this exact transformer backend production-grade:
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:45:A smaller transformer such as 2 layers, `d_model=128..192`, and `ff_dim=512..768` could reduce GEMM/scratch cost while staying a transformer backend. It requires relaxing current exporter/runtime shape checks, which currently reject non-4-layer/non-1024-FF models.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:47:Risk: medium quality loss. Runtime friendliness: medium. This is valid only as an explicit transformer backend variant, not a fallback to MLP.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:57:This is not an MLP fallback. It would be a new explicit piece-token backend with transformer-like token processing but no softmax attention. It is mostly GEMM plus fused bias/activation/residual kernels and removes attention score/probability scratch.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:69:Do current-backend optimization first: benchmark, remove redundant clears, remove global attention scratch, tune transformer-specific batching/concurrency, and then consider smaller transformer or piece-mixer variants. Do not train or wire an MLP distillation path for this track.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:72:After the native CUDA/CUTLASS v19 extended 2xT4 sweep, the best verified native aggregate is `1224735.7` candidates/s, about `0.971x` of the PyTorch `batch_process` reference aggregate. The native experiments remain archived under `docs/archive/stream1_transformer_native/`.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:74:The transformer direction is now three explicit execution backends for the same `piece_transformer` model family: `pytorch`, `libtorch`, and `native_cutlass`. This does not change the manifest model backend, does not distill to MLP, and does not add fallback behavior. Default builds remain Torch-free; the C++ LibTorch benchmark/tool target is enabled only with `BEAM_ENABLE_LIBTORCH_STREAM1=ON`.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:76:Production dispatcher integration remains a separate step for non-native paths because the current dispatcher captures Stream1+Stream2 CUDA Graph templates. LibTorch op dispatch must either be proven graph-capture safe with stable allocations or run through a named non-graph Stream1 execution path. The PyTorch path stays a Python/Kaggle execution backend unless an explicit bridge is designed.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:77:## 2026-07-04 CUTLASS Profiling Update
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:79:A later Docker Nsight pass compared the explicit C++ LibTorch/cuBLAS path against the native CUTLASS Stream1 transformer path on local RTX 3070. The native CUTLASS backend is already the practical cuBLAS replacement route for production: local b384/c1 reached `871235.6` candidates/s and b512/c2 reached `991974.7`, versus local LibTorch eager `578741`. Nsight Compute 2025.1.1 confirmed both cuBLASLt and CUTLASS sampled GEMMs use HMMA tensor cores.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:81:The current bottleneck is not missing tensor-core use. Native CUTLASS sampled kernels are around `40-43%` SM throughput and `62-70%` DRAM throughput, so the next work should tune transformer-specific GEMM/epilogue/layout/LayerNorm structure. A wider `128x128x32` QKV/FF1 tile regressed versus the current `128x64x32` tile and was reverted. Evidence: `test_results/stream1_transformer_cutlass_vs_libtorch_profile_2026-07-04.md`.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:84:The backend registry is `tools/stream1_transformer_backends.py`; details are in `docs/stream1_transformer_backends.md`.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:86:The three supported backend names are:
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:88:- `pytorch`: Python/Torch execution, including the Kaggle notebook path and exported-weight Torch benchmark.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:89:- `libtorch`: explicit C++ LibTorch execution over exported weights.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:90:- `native_cutlass`: native C++/CUDA/CUTLASS execution through the existing Stream1 transformer runtime.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:95:The three Stream1 `piece_transformer` backends now share a parity launcher contract. PyTorch and LibTorch benchmarks emit `checksum` and `first_score_keys`, and the native CUTLASS transformer microbenchmark emits the same fields while optionally using `BEAM_STREAM1_SYNTHETIC_STATES=1` for deterministic synthetic inputs.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:97:`tools/stream1_transformer_parity.py` is the correctness gate for comparing explicit backends on one synthetic batch. It supports a dry-run mode for clean checkout validation and an execution mode for GPU hosts with exported weights and built backend binaries. This is only a verification layer; production dispatch still selects one backend explicitly and must not silently fall back.
docs\transformer_inference_architecture.md:100:The backend parity contract now includes `score_key_digest`, a deterministic FNV-1a 64-bit digest over all quantized score keys in batch-major, move-major order. The digest is emitted by the PyTorch benchmark, the C++ LibTorch benchmark, and the native CUTLASS transformer microbenchmark. Parity execution should treat digest mismatch as a hard correctness failure; checksum and first-row score keys are secondary diagnostics.

39:def product(shape: Iterable[int]) -> int:

45:
46:def load_file_tensor(path: Path, shape: Tuple[int, ...], dtype: torch.dtype, device: torch.device) -> torch.Tensor:
47:    expected = product(shape)
48:    tensor = torch.from_file(str(path), shared=False, size=expected, dtype=dtype)
49:    if tensor.numel() != expected:
50:        raise ValueError(f"{path}: expected {expected} values, got {tensor.numel()}")
51:    return tensor.reshape(shape).to(device=device, non_blocking=False)

58:def score_key_digest(keys: torch.Tensor) -> int:

63:        for shift in (0, 8, 16, 24):
64:            digest ^= (value >> shift) & 0xFF
65:            digest = (digest * FNV64_PRIME) & FNV64_MASK
66:    return digest

91:        if self.seq_len != self.num_pieces + 1:
92:            raise ValueError("seq_len must equal num_pieces + cls token")
93:        if self.d_model != self.nhead * self.head_dim:
94:            raise ValueError("d_model must equal nhead * head_dim")
95:        if self.output_dim != self.move_count:
96:            raise ValueError("output_dim must equal move_count")
97:
98:        suffix = ".fp16"
99:        half = torch.float16
100:        self.fast_slot_projected = load_file_tensor(
101:            weight_dir / f"fast_slot_projected{suffix}",
102:            (self.max_piece_size, self.num_classes, self.d_model),
103:            half,
104:            device,
105:        )
106:        self.fast_piece_static = load_file_tensor(
107:            weight_dir / f"fast_piece_static{suffix}",
108:            (self.num_pieces, self.d_model),
109:            half,
110:            device,
111:        )
112:        self.cls_token = load_file_tensor(weight_dir / f"cls_token{suffix}", (self.d_model,), half, device)
113:        self.input_ln_gamma = load_file_tensor(weight_dir / f"input_ln_gamma{suffix}", (self.d_model,), half, device)
114:        self.input_ln_beta = load_file_tensor(weight_dir / f"input_ln_beta{suffix}", (self.d_model,), half, device)
115:        self.output_ln_gamma = load_file_tensor(weight_dir / f"output_ln_gamma{suffix}", (self.d_model,), half, device)
116:        self.output_ln_beta = load_file_tensor(weight_dir / f"output_ln_beta{suffix}", (self.d_model,), half, device)
117:        self.output_weight = load_file_tensor(
118:            weight_dir / f"output_weight_hxk{suffix}",
119:            (self.d_model, self.output_dim),
120:            half,
121:            device,
122:        )
123:        self.output_bias = load_file_tensor(weight_dir / f"output_bias{suffix}", (self.output_dim,), half, device)
124:        self.piece_positions = load_file_tensor(
125:            weight_dir / "piece_positions.u16",
126:            (self.num_pieces, self.max_piece_size),
127:            torch.int16,
128:            device,
129:        ).long()
130:        self.piece_mask = load_file_tensor(
131:            weight_dir / "piece_mask.u8",
132:            (self.num_pieces, self.max_piece_size),
133:            torch.uint8,
134:            device,
135:        ).bool()
136:
137:        self.blocks: List[Dict[str, torch.Tensor]] = []
138:        for block in range(self.num_layers):
139:            prefix = f"block{block}"
140:            self.blocks.append(
141:                {
142:                    "ln1_gamma": load_file_tensor(weight_dir / f"{prefix}_ln1_gamma{suffix}", (self.d_model,), half, device),
143:                    "ln1_beta": load_file_tensor(weight_dir / f"{prefix}_ln1_beta{suffix}", (self.d_model,), half, device),
144:                    "qkv_weight": load_file_tensor(
145:                        weight_dir / f"{prefix}_attn_qkv_weight_hxk{suffix}",
146:                        (self.d_model, 3 * self.d_model),
147:                        half,
148:                        device,
149:                    ),
150:                    "qkv_bias": load_file_tensor(
151:                        weight_dir / f"{prefix}_attn_qkv_bias{suffix}",
152:                        (3 * self.d_model,),
153:                        half,
154:                        device,
155:                    ),
156:                    "attn_out_weight": load_file_tensor(
157:                        weight_dir / f"{prefix}_attn_out_weight_hxk{suffix}",
158:                        (self.d_model, self.d_model),
159:                        half,
160:                        device,
161:                    ),
162:                    "attn_out_bias": load_file_tensor(
163:                        weight_dir / f"{prefix}_attn_out_bias{suffix}",
164:                        (self.d_model,),
165:                        half,
166:                        device,
167:                    ),
168:                    "ln2_gamma": load_file_tensor(weight_dir / f"{prefix}_ln2_gamma{suffix}", (self.d_model,), half, device),
169:                    "ln2_beta": load_file_tensor(weight_dir / f"{prefix}_ln2_beta{suffix}", (self.d_model,), half, device),
170:                    "ff1_weight": load_file_tensor(
171:                        weight_dir / f"{prefix}_ff1_weight_hxk{suffix}",
172:                        (self.d_model, self.ff_dim),
173:                        half,
174:                        device,
175:                    ),
176:                    "ff1_bias": load_file_tensor(weight_dir / f"{prefix}_ff1_bias{suffix}", (self.ff_dim,), half, device),
177:                    "ff2_weight": load_file_tensor(
178:                        weight_dir / f"{prefix}_ff2_weight_hxk{suffix}",
179:                        (self.ff_dim, self.d_model),
180:                        half,
181:                        device,
182:                    ),
183:                    "ff2_bias": load_file_tensor(weight_dir / f"{prefix}_ff2_bias{suffix}", (self.d_model,), half, device),
184:                }
185:            )
186:
187:    def layer_norm(self, x: torch.Tensor, gamma: torch.Tensor, beta: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
188:        return F.layer_norm(x, (self.d_model,), gamma, beta, eps=1.0e-5)
189:
190:    def build_tokens(self, states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
191:        states = states[:, : self.state_len].long()
192:        batch = states.shape[0]
193:        pieces = self.fast_piece_static.unsqueeze(0).expand(batch, -1, -1).clone()
194:        for slot in range(self.max_piece_size):
195:            mask = self.piece_mask[:, slot]
196:            if not bool(mask.any().item()):
197:                continue
198:            positions = self.piece_positions[:, slot]
199:            state_values = states[:, positions]
200:            slot_table = self.fast_slot_projected[slot]
201:            pieces[:, mask, :] += slot_table[state_values[:, mask]]
202:        cls = self.cls_token.reshape(1, 1, self.d_model).expand(batch, 1, self.d_model)
203:        return torch.cat((cls, pieces), dim=1)
204:
205:    def forward(self, states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
206:        x = self.build_tokens(states)
207:        x = self.layer_norm(x, self.input_ln_gamma, self.input_ln_beta)
208:        batch = x.shape[0]
209:        for block in self.blocks:
210:            y = self.layer_norm(x, block["ln1_gamma"], block["ln1_beta"])
211:            qkv = y.matmul(block["qkv_weight"]) + block["qkv_bias"]
212:            qkv = qkv.reshape(batch, self.seq_len, 3, self.nhead, self.head_dim)
213:            q = qkv[:, :, 0, :, :].permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous()
214:            k = qkv[:, :, 1, :, :].permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous()
215:            v = qkv[:, :, 2, :, :].permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous()
216:            attn = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, dropout_p=0.0, is_causal=False)
217:            context = attn.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).reshape(batch, self.seq_len, self.d_model)
218:            x = x + context.matmul(block["attn_out_weight"]) + block["attn_out_bias"]
219:
220:            y = self.layer_norm(x, block["ln2_gamma"], block["ln2_beta"])
221:            y = F.silu(y.matmul(block["ff1_weight"]) + block["ff1_bias"])
222:            x = x + y.matmul(block["ff2_weight"]) + block["ff2_bias"]
223:
224:        cls = self.layer_norm(x[:, 0, :], self.output_ln_gamma, self.output_ln_beta)
225:        return cls.matmul(self.output_weight) + self.output_bias
226:
227:
228:def validate_reference(model: PieceTransformerTorch, reference_path: Path, tolerance: int) -> Tuple[str, int, int]:
229:    reference = json.loads(reference_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
230:    states = torch.tensor(reference["states"], dtype=torch.uint8, device=model.device)
231:    expected_scores = torch.tensor(reference["scores_fp32"], dtype=torch.float32, device=model.device)
232:    with torch.inference_mode():
233:        logits = model.forward(states)
234:        actual_keys = score_keys(logits)
235:    expected_keys = score_keys(expected_scores)
236:    max_error = int((actual_keys - expected_keys).abs().max().item())
237:    status = "pass" if max_error <= tolerance else "fail"
238:    return status, int(states.shape[0]), max_error
239:
240:
241:def make_states(batch: int, state_len: int, num_classes: int, device: torch.device) -> torch.Tensor:
242:    values = torch.arange(batch * state_len, device=device, dtype=torch.int64)
243:    values = (values.reshape(batch, state_len) * 17 + 23) % num_classes
244:    return values.to(torch.uint8)
245:
246:
247:def benchmark_batch(
248:    model: PieceTransformerTorch,
249:    batch: int,
250:    warmup: int,
251:    iters: int,
252:) -> Dict[str, object]:
253:    states = make_states(batch, model.state_len, model.num_classes, model.device)
254:    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
255:    torch.cuda.reset_peak_memory_stats(model.device)
256:    with torch.inference_mode():
257:        for _ in range(warmup):
258:            logits = model.forward(states)
259:        torch.cuda.synchronize(model.device)
260:        start = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)
261:        end = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)
262:        start.record()
263:        for _ in range(iters):
264:            logits = model.forward(states)
265:        end.record()
266:        torch.cuda.synchronize(model.device)
267:    elapsed_ms = float(start.elapsed_time(end))
268:    keys = score_keys(logits)
269:    checksum = int(keys.sum().item())
270:    score_digest = score_key_digest(keys)
271:    first_score_keys = ",".join(str(int(x)) for x in keys[0].detach().cpu().tolist()) if batch > 0 else ""
272:    del logits
273:    parents_per_s = (batch * iters) / (elapsed_ms / 1000.0)
274:    candidates_per_s = parents_per_s * model.move_count
275:    peak_gib = torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated(model.device) / (1024.0**3)
276:    return {
277:        "batch": batch,
278:        "iters": iters,
279:        "elapsed_ms": elapsed_ms,
280:        "ms_per_iter": elapsed_ms / iters,
281:        "parents_per_s": parents_per_s,
282:        "candidates_per_s": candidates_per_s,
283:        "peak_mem_gib": peak_gib,
284:        "checksum": checksum,
285:        "score_key_digest": score_digest,
286:        "first_score_keys": first_score_keys,
287:        "status": "ok",
288:    }
289:
290:
291:def write_report(path: Path, rows: List[Dict[str, object]], metadata: Dict[str, object]) -> None:
292:    best = max((row for row in rows if row.get("status") == "ok"), key=lambda r: float(r["candidates_per_s"]), default=None)
293:    lines = [
294:        "# Stream1 Piece-Transformer PyTorch Benchmark",
295:        "",
296:        f"- torch_version={metadata['torch_version']}",
297:        f"- cuda_version={metadata['cuda_version']}",
298:        f"- device={metadata['device_name']}",
299:        f"- weight_dir={metadata['weight_dir']}",
300:        f"- reference_status={metadata['reference_status']}",
301:        f"- reference_rows={metadata['reference_rows']}",
302:        f"- reference_max_abs_score_key_error={metadata['reference_max_abs_score_key_error']}",
303:        "",
304:        "| batch | iters | ms/iter | parents/s | candidates/s | peak GiB | checksum | score_key_digest | status |",
305:        "|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|",
306:    ]
307:    for row in rows:
308:        if row.get("status") == "ok":
309:            lines.append(
310:                "| {batch} | {iters} | {ms_per_iter:.4f} | {parents_per_s:.1f} | {candidates_per_s:.1f} | {peak_mem_gib:.3f} | {checksum} | {score_key_digest} | ok |".format(
311:                    **row
312:                )
313:            )
314:        else:
315:            lines.append(f"| {row['batch']} | - | - | - | - | - | - | - | {row['status']} |")
316:    if best is not None:
317:        lines.extend(
318:            [
319:                "",
320:                f"best_batch={best['batch']}",
321:                f"best_candidates_per_s={float(best['candidates_per_s']):.1f}",
322:            ]
323:        )
324:    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
325:    path.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
326:
327:
328:def main() -> None:
329:    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
330:    parser.add_argument("--weight-dir", type=Path, default=Path("test_results/stream1_transformer_reference/weights_fp16"))
331:    parser.add_argument("--reference-json", type=Path, default=Path("test_results/stream1_transformer_reference/reference.json"))
332:    parser.add_argument("--batch-sizes", default="128,256,512,1024,2048,4096")
333:    parser.add_argument("--warmup", type=int, default=8)
334:    parser.add_argument("--iters", type=int, default=30)
335:    parser.add_argument("--reference-tolerance", type=int, default=3072)
336:    parser.add_argument("--skip-reference", action="store_true", help="Skip reference JSON validation for synthetic benchmark/parity runs.")
337:    parser.add_argument("--report", type=Path, default=Path("test_results/stream1_transformer_torch_benchmark_2026-06-30.md"))
338:    args = parser.parse_args()
339:
340:    if not torch.cuda.is_available():
341:        raise RuntimeError("CUDA is required for this benchmark")
342:    torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = False
343:    torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32 = False
344:    device = torch.device("cuda")
345:    model = PieceTransformerTorch(args.weight_dir, device)
346:    if args.skip_reference:
347:        ref_status, ref_rows, ref_error = "skip", 0, 0
348:    else:
349:        ref_status, ref_rows, ref_error = validate_reference(model, args.reference_json, args.reference_tolerance)
350:        if ref_status != "pass":
351:            raise RuntimeError(f"reference validation failed: max_abs_score_key_error={ref_error}")
352:
353:    rows: List[Dict[str, object]] = []
354:    for batch in parse_batch_sizes(args.batch_sizes):
355:        try:
356:            row = benchmark_batch(model, batch, args.warmup, args.iters)
357:        except RuntimeError as exc:
358:            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
359:            rows.append({"batch": batch, "status": type(exc).__name__ + ": " + str(exc).splitlines()[0][:160]})
360:            continue
361:        rows.append(row)
362:        print(
363:            "torch_stream1_transformer"
364:            f" batch={row['batch']}"
365:            f" ms_per_iter={row['ms_per_iter']:.4f}"
366:            f" parents_per_s={row['parents_per_s']:.1f}"
367:            f" candidates_per_s={row['candidates_per_s']:.1f}"
368:            f" peak_mem_gib={row['peak_mem_gib']:.3f}"
369:            f" checksum={row['checksum']}"
370:            f" score_key_digest={row['score_key_digest']}"
371:            f" first_score_keys={row['first_score_keys']}",
372:            flush=True,
373:        )
374:
375:    metadata = {
376:        "torch_version": torch.__version__,
377:        "cuda_version": torch.version.cuda,
378:        "device_name": torch.cuda.get_device_name(device),
379:        "weight_dir": str(args.weight_dir),
380:        "reference_status": ref_status,
381:        "reference_rows": ref_rows,
382:        "reference_max_abs_score_key_error": ref_error,
383:    }
384:    write_report(args.report, rows, metadata)
385:    with args.report.with_suffix(".tsv").open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
386:        fieldnames = ["batch", "iters", "elapsed_ms", "ms_per_iter", "parents_per_s", "candidates_per_s", "peak_mem_gib", "checksum", "score_key_digest", "first_score_keys", "status"]
387:        writer = csv.DictWriter(fh, fieldnames=fieldnames, delimiter="\t", extrasaction="ignore")
388:        writer.writeheader()
389:        writer.writerows(rows)
390:    print(f"torch_stream1_transformer_report={args.report}")
391:

394:    main()

27:inline std::string read_text_exact(const fs::path& path) {
28:    std::ifstream in(path, std::ios::binary);
29:    if (!in) {
30:        throw std::runtime_error("failed to open text file: " + path.string());
31:    }

35:inline std::vector<std::uint8_t> read_binary_exact(const fs::path& path, std::uint64_t bytes) {
36:    std::ifstream in(path, std::ios::binary);
37:    if (!in) {
38:        throw std::runtime_error("failed to open binary file: " + path.string());
39:    }
40:    std::vector<std::uint8_t> out(static_cast<std::size_t>(bytes));
41:    in.read(reinterpret_cast<char*>(out.data()), static_cast<std::streamsize>(out.size()));
42:    if (in.gcount() != static_cast<std::streamsize>(out.size())) {
43:        throw std::runtime_error("short read: " + path.string());
44:    }
45:    char extra = 0;
46:    if (in.read(&extra, 1)) {
47:        throw std::runtime_error("file larger than expected: " + path.string());
48:    }
49:    return out;
50:}

52:inline std::string manifest_string(const std::string& text, const std::string& key) {
53:    const std::regex re("\"" + key + "\"\\s*:\\s*\"([^\"]*)\"");
54:    std::smatch match;
55:    if (!std::regex_search(text, match, re)) {
56:        throw std::runtime_error("manifest missing string key: " + key);
57:    }
58:    return match[1].str();
59:}
60:
61:inline std::uint32_t manifest_u32(const std::string& text, const std::string& key) {
62:    const std::regex re("\"" + key + "\"\\s*:\\s*([0-9]+)");
63:    std::smatch match;
64:    if (!std::regex_search(text, match, re)) {
65:        throw std::runtime_error("manifest missing integer key: " + key);
66:    }
67:    const unsigned long value = std::stoul(match[1].str());
68:    if (value > UINT32_MAX) {
69:        throw std::runtime_error("manifest integer key too large: " + key);
70:    }
71:    return static_cast<std::uint32_t>(value);
72:}

74:inline std::uint32_t manifest_u32_any(const std::string& text, const std::string& key, const std::string& alternate) {
75:    const std::regex re("\"" + key + "\"\\s*:\\s*([0-9]+)");
76:    if (std::regex_search(text, re)) {
77:        return manifest_u32(text, key);
78:    }
79:    return manifest_u32(text, alternate);
80:}

82:inline std::vector<std::int64_t> parse_csv_i64(const std::string& text) {
83:    std::vector<std::int64_t> values;
84:    std::size_t start = 0;
85:    while (start <= text.size()) {
86:        const std::size_t comma = text.find(',', start);
87:        const std::string token = text.substr(start, comma == std::string::npos ? std::string::npos : comma - start);
88:        if (!token.empty()) {
89:            values.push_back(std::stoll(token));
90:        }
91:        if (comma == std::string::npos) {
92:            break;
93:        }
94:        start = comma + 1;
95:    }
96:    if (values.empty()) {
97:        throw std::runtime_error("empty integer csv list");
98:    }
99:    return values;
100:}

102:inline std::int64_t product(const std::vector<std::int64_t>& shape) {
103:    return std::accumulate(shape.begin(), shape.end(), std::int64_t{1}, std::multiplies<std::int64_t>());
104:}

106:inline torch::Tensor load_tensor(
107:    const fs::path& path,
108:    const std::vector<std::int64_t>& shape,
109:    torch::ScalarType dtype,
110:    const torch::Device& device) {
111:    const std::int64_t values = product(shape);
112:    const std::uint64_t element_bytes = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(torch::elementSize(dtype));
113:    std::vector<std::uint8_t> bytes = read_binary_exact(path, static_cast<std::uint64_t>(values) * element_bytes);
114:    torch::Tensor cpu = torch::from_blob(
115:        bytes.data(),
116:        shape,
117:        torch::TensorOptions().dtype(dtype).device(torch::kCPU));
118:    return cpu.clone().to(device, /*non_blocking=*/false);
119:}

124:    torch::ScalarType dtype,
125:    const torch::Device& device) {
126:    return load_tensor(path, hxk_shape, dtype, device).transpose(0, 1).contiguous();
127:}
128:inline torch::Tensor make_states(
129:    std::int64_t batch,
130:    std::int64_t state_len,
131:    std::int64_t num_classes,
132:    const torch::Device& device) {
133:    torch::Tensor values = torch::arange(batch * state_len, torch::TensorOptions().dtype(torch::kInt64).device(device));
134:    values = (values.view({batch, state_len}) * 17 + 23).remainder(num_classes);
135:    return values.to(torch::kUInt8);
136:}
137:
138:inline torch::Tensor score_keys(const torch::Tensor& logits) {
139:    return torch::round(torch::clamp(logits.to(torch::kFloat32), 0.0, kScoreMaxQ) * kScoreScale).to(torch::kInt64);
140:}
141:
142:struct TransformerBlock {
143:    torch::Tensor ln1_gamma;
144:    torch::Tensor ln1_beta;
145:    torch::Tensor qkv_weight_kxh;
146:    torch::Tensor qkv_bias;
147:    torch::Tensor attn_out_weight_kxh;
148:    torch::Tensor attn_out_bias;
149:    torch::Tensor ln2_gamma;
150:    torch::Tensor ln2_beta;
151:    torch::Tensor ff1_weight_kxh;
152:    torch::Tensor ff1_bias;
153:    torch::Tensor ff2_weight_kxh;
154:    torch::Tensor ff2_bias;
155:};
156:
157:struct PieceTransformerLibTorch {
158:    fs::path weight_dir;
159:    torch::Device device;
160:    torch::ScalarType dtype = torch::kFloat16;
161:    std::string dtype_suffix;
162:    std::uint32_t state_len = 0;
163:    std::uint32_t num_classes = 0;
164:    std::uint32_t move_count = 0;
165:    std::uint32_t output_dim = 0;
166:    std::uint32_t num_pieces = 0;
167:    std::uint32_t max_piece_size = 0;
168:    std::uint32_t seq_len = 0;
169:    std::uint32_t d_model = 0;
170:    std::uint32_t nhead = 0;
171:    std::uint32_t head_dim = 0;
172:    std::uint32_t num_layers = 0;
173:    std::uint32_t ff_dim = 0;
174:
175:    torch::Tensor fast_slot_projected;
176:    torch::Tensor fast_piece_static;
177:    torch::Tensor cls_token;
178:    torch::Tensor input_ln_gamma;
179:    torch::Tensor input_ln_beta;
180:    torch::Tensor output_ln_gamma;
181:    torch::Tensor output_ln_beta;
182:    torch::Tensor output_weight_kxh;
183:    torch::Tensor output_bias;
184:    torch::Tensor piece_positions;
185:    torch::Tensor piece_mask;
186:    std::vector<TransformerBlock> blocks;
187:
188:    PieceTransformerLibTorch(fs::path dir, torch::Device target_device)
189:        : weight_dir(std::move(dir)), device(std::move(target_device)) {
190:        const std::string manifest = read_text_exact(weight_dir / "manifest.json");
191:        if (manifest_string(manifest, "backend") != "piece_transformer") {
192:            throw std::runtime_error("manifest backend must be piece_transformer");
193:        }
194:        const std::string manifest_dtype = manifest_string(manifest, "dtype");
195:        if (manifest_dtype == "fp16") {
196:            dtype = torch::kFloat16;
197:            dtype_suffix = ".fp16";
198:        } else if (manifest_dtype == "bf16") {
199:            dtype = torch::kBFloat16;
200:            dtype_suffix = ".bf16";
201:        } else {
202:            throw std::runtime_error("manifest dtype must be fp16 or bf16");
203:        }
204:        state_len = manifest_u32(manifest, "state_len");
205:        num_classes = manifest_u32(manifest, "num_classes");
206:        move_count = manifest_u32(manifest, "move_count");
207:        output_dim = manifest_u32(manifest, "output_dim");
208:        num_pieces = manifest_u32(manifest, "num_pieces");
209:        max_piece_size = manifest_u32(manifest, "max_piece_size");
210:        seq_len = manifest_u32(manifest, "seq_len");
211:        d_model = manifest_u32(manifest, "d_model");
212:        nhead = manifest_u32(manifest, "nhead");
213:        head_dim = manifest_u32(manifest, "head_dim");
214:        num_layers = manifest_u32_any(manifest, "num_layers", "transformer_layers");
215:        ff_dim = manifest_u32(manifest, "ff_dim");
216:        if (seq_len != num_pieces + 1U || d_model != nhead * head_dim || output_dim != move_count) {
217:            throw std::runtime_error("invalid piece_transformer manifest dimensions");
218:        }
219:        if (manifest_string(manifest, "activation") != "silu" ||
220:            manifest_string(manifest, "pooling") != "cls" ||
221:            manifest_string(manifest, "piece_layout") != "p900" ||
222:            manifest_string(manifest, "piece_embed_mode") != "full_s120") {
223:            throw std::runtime_error("unsupported piece_transformer manifest contract");
224:        }
225:
226:        fast_slot_projected = load_tensor(
227:            weight_dir / ("fast_slot_projected" + dtype_suffix),
228:            {max_piece_size, num_classes, d_model},
229:            dtype,
230:            device);
231:        fast_piece_static =
232:            load_tensor(weight_dir / ("fast_piece_static" + dtype_suffix), {num_pieces, d_model}, dtype, device);
233:        cls_token = load_tensor(weight_dir / ("cls_token" + dtype_suffix), {d_model}, dtype, device);
234:        input_ln_gamma = load_tensor(weight_dir / ("input_ln_gamma" + dtype_suffix), {d_model}, dtype, device);
235:        input_ln_beta = load_tensor(weight_dir / ("input_ln_beta" + dtype_suffix), {d_model}, dtype, device);
236:        output_ln_gamma = load_tensor(weight_dir / ("output_ln_gamma" + dtype_suffix), {d_model}, dtype, device);
237:        output_ln_beta = load_tensor(weight_dir / ("output_ln_beta" + dtype_suffix), {d_model}, dtype, device);
238:        output_weight_kxh = load_linear_weight_kxh(weight_dir / ("output_weight_hxk" + dtype_suffix), {d_model, output_dim}, dtype, device);
239:        output_bias = load_tensor(weight_dir / ("output_bias" + dtype_suffix), {output_dim}, dtype, device);
240:        piece_positions =
241:            load_tensor(weight_dir / "piece_positions.u16", {num_pieces, max_piece_size}, torch::kInt16, device)
242:                .to(torch::kLong);
243:        piece_mask = load_tensor(weight_dir / "piece_mask.u8", {num_pieces, max_piece_size}, torch::kUInt8, device)
244:                         .to(torch::kBool);
245:        blocks.reserve(num_layers);
246:        for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < num_layers; ++i) {
247:            const std::string prefix = "block" + std::to_string(i);
248:            blocks.push_back(TransformerBlock{
249:                load_tensor(weight_dir / (prefix + "_ln1_gamma" + dtype_suffix), {d_model}, dtype, device),
250:                load_tensor(weight_dir / (prefix + "_ln1_beta" + dtype_suffix), {d_model}, dtype, device),
251:                load_linear_weight_kxh(weight_dir / (prefix + "_attn_qkv_weight_hxk" + dtype_suffix), {d_model, 3U * d_model}, dtype, device),
252:                load_tensor(weight_dir / (prefix + "_attn_qkv_bias" + dtype_suffix), {3U * d_model}, dtype, device),
253:                load_linear_weight_kxh(weight_dir / (prefix + "_attn_out_weight_hxk" + dtype_suffix), {d_model, d_model}, dtype, device),
254:                load_tensor(weight_dir / (prefix + "_attn_out_bias" + dtype_suffix), {d_model}, dtype, device),
255:                load_tensor(weight_dir / (prefix + "_ln2_gamma" + dtype_suffix), {d_model}, dtype, device),
256:                load_tensor(weight_dir / (prefix + "_ln2_beta" + dtype_suffix), {d_model}, dtype, device),
257:                load_linear_weight_kxh(weight_dir / (prefix + "_ff1_weight_hxk" + dtype_suffix), {d_model, ff_dim}, dtype, device),
258:                load_tensor(weight_dir / (prefix + "_ff1_bias" + dtype_suffix), {ff_dim}, dtype, device),
259:                load_linear_weight_kxh(weight_dir / (prefix + "_ff2_weight_hxk" + dtype_suffix), {ff_dim, d_model}, dtype, device),
260:                load_tensor(weight_dir / (prefix + "_ff2_bias" + dtype_suffix), {d_model}, dtype, device),
261:            });
262:        }
263:    }
264:
265:    torch::Tensor layer_norm(const torch::Tensor& x, const torch::Tensor& gamma, const torch::Tensor& beta) const {
266:        return torch::layer_norm(x, {static_cast<std::int64_t>(d_model)}, gamma, beta, 1.0e-5, false);
267:    }
268:
269:    torch::Tensor linear_kxh(const torch::Tensor& x, const torch::Tensor& weight_kxh, const torch::Tensor& bias) const {
270:        return at::linear(x, weight_kxh, bias);
271:    }
272:    torch::Tensor build_tokens(const torch::Tensor& state_u8) const {
273:        const torch::Tensor states = state_u8.index({torch::indexing::Slice(), torch::indexing::Slice(0, state_len)})
274:                                         .to(torch::kLong);
275:        const std::int64_t batch = states.size(0);
276:        torch::Tensor pieces = fast_piece_static.unsqueeze(0).expand({batch, -1, -1}).clone();
277:        for (std::uint32_t slot = 0; slot < max_piece_size; ++slot) {
278:            torch::Tensor positions = piece_positions.index({torch::indexing::Slice(), static_cast<std::int64_t>(slot)});
279:            torch::Tensor state_values = states.index_select(1, positions).reshape({-1});
280:            torch::Tensor gathered = fast_slot_projected.index({static_cast<std::int64_t>(slot)})
281:                                         .index_select(0, state_values)
282:                                         .view({batch, static_cast<std::int64_t>(num_pieces), static_cast<std::int64_t>(d_model)});
283:            torch::Tensor mask = piece_mask.index({torch::indexing::Slice(), static_cast<std::int64_t>(slot)})
284:                                     .to(dtype)
285:                                     .view({1, static_cast<std::int64_t>(num_pieces), 1});
286:            pieces = pieces + gathered * mask;
287:        }
288:        torch::Tensor cls = cls_token.view({1, 1, static_cast<std::int64_t>(d_model)}).expand({batch, 1, -1});
289:        return torch::cat({cls, pieces}, 1);
290:    }
291:
292:    torch::Tensor forward(const torch::Tensor& state_u8) const {
293:        const std::int64_t batch = state_u8.size(0);
294:        torch::Tensor x = layer_norm(build_tokens(state_u8), input_ln_gamma, input_ln_beta);
295:        for (const TransformerBlock& block : blocks) {
296:            torch::Tensor y = layer_norm(x, block.ln1_gamma, block.ln1_beta);
297:            torch::Tensor qkv = linear_kxh(y, block.qkv_weight_kxh, block.qkv_bias);
298:            qkv = qkv.view({batch,
299:                            static_cast<std::int64_t>(seq_len),
300:                            3,
301:                            static_cast<std::int64_t>(nhead),
302:                            static_cast<std::int64_t>(head_dim)});
303:            torch::Tensor q = qkv.select(2, 0).permute({0, 2, 1, 3});
304:            torch::Tensor k = qkv.select(2, 1).permute({0, 2, 1, 3});
305:            torch::Tensor v = qkv.select(2, 2).permute({0, 2, 1, 3});
306:            torch::Tensor attn = at::scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, std::nullopt, 0.0, false, std::nullopt, false);
307:            torch::Tensor context = attn.permute({0, 2, 1, 3})
308:                                        .reshape({batch,
309:                                                  static_cast<std::int64_t>(seq_len),
310:                                                  static_cast<std::int64_t>(d_model)});
311:            x = x + linear_kxh(context, block.attn_out_weight_kxh, block.attn_out_bias);
312:
313:            y = layer_norm(x, block.ln2_gamma, block.ln2_beta);
314:            y = linear_kxh(y, block.ff1_weight_kxh, block.ff1_bias);
315:            y = y * torch::sigmoid(y);
316:            x = x + linear_kxh(y, block.ff2_weight_kxh, block.ff2_bias);
317:        }
318:        torch::Tensor cls = layer_norm(x.index({torch::indexing::Slice(), 0, torch::indexing::Slice()}), output_ln_gamma, output_ln_beta);
319:        return linear_kxh(cls, output_weight_kxh, output_bias);
320:    }
321:};
322:
323:
324:inline void synchronize_if_cuda(const torch::Device& device) {
325:    if (device.is_cuda()) {
326:        torch::cuda::synchronize(device.index());
327:    }
328:}
329:
330:} // namespace beam::stream1_libtorch

96:std::uint64_t score_key_digest(const torch::Tensor& keys) {
97:    torch::Tensor flat = keys.to(torch::kCPU).to(torch::kInt64).contiguous().view({-1});
98:    auto accessor = flat.accessor<std::int64_t, 1>();
99:    std::uint64_t digest = FNV64_OFFSET;

101:        const std::uint32_t value = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(accessor[i]);
102:        for (int shift = 0; shift < 32; shift += 8) {
103:            digest ^= static_cast<std::uint64_t>((value >> shift) & 0xFFU);
104:            digest *= FNV64_PRIME;
105:        }
106:    }
107:    return digest;
108:}

15:ScoreSummary summarize_score_buffers(

37:                if (move != 0) {

136:        ? static_cast<std::uint32_t>(parse_u64(only_concurrency_env, "BEAM_STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_CONCURRENCY"))
137:        : 0U;
138:
139:    report << "## Stream1 Piece Transformer\n\n";
140:    report << "- graph_bench=" << (graph_bench ? 1 : 0) << "\n";
141:    if (only_b_micro != 0U || only_concurrency != 0U) {
142:        report << "- filter_b_micro=" << only_b_micro << "\n";
143:        report << "- filter_concurrency=" << only_concurrency << "\n\n";
144:    }
145:    report << "| b_micro | concurrency | rows_per_launch_group | ms_per_launch_group | parents_per_sec | candidates_per_sec | checksum | score_key_digest | first_score_keys | scratch_bytes |\n";
146:    report << "|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|---:|\n";
147:    for (std::uint32_t b_micro : TRANSFORMER_B_MICRO_SWEEP) {
148:        for (std::uint32_t concurrent : TRANSFORMER_STREAM1_CONCURRENCY_SWEEP) {
149:            if ((only_b_micro != 0U && b_micro != only_b_micro) ||
150:                (only_concurrency != 0U && concurrent != only_concurrency)) {
151:                continue;
152:            }
153:            const std::uint64_t rows_per_launch_group = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(b_micro) * concurrent;
154:            if (rows_per_launch_group > max_states) {
155:                report << "|" << b_micro << "|" << concurrent << "|" << rows_per_launch_group
156:                       << "|skip|skip|skip|skip|skip|skip|0: exceeds prepared state batch|\n";
157:                std::cout << "stream1_transformer_micro_skip"
158:                          << " b_micro=" << b_micro
159:                          << " concurrency=" << concurrent
160:                          << " reason=exceeds_prepared_state_batch\n";
161:                continue;
162:            }
163:
164:            const std::uint64_t scratch_bytes =
165:                stream1_weights::stream1_scratch_bytes(model, b_micro, concurrent);
166:            const std::uint64_t io_bytes =
167:                rows_per_launch_group *
168:                (sizeof(std::uint64_t) + sizeof(std::uint32_t) +
169:                 static_cast<std::uint64_t>(MOVE_COUNT) * sizeof(std::uint32_t));
170:            std::size_t free_bytes = 0;
171:            std::size_t total_bytes = 0;
172:            BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_bytes, &total_bytes));
173:            constexpr std::uint64_t safety_margin_bytes = 256ULL * 1024ULL * 1024ULL;
174:            if (scratch_bytes + io_bytes + safety_margin_bytes > free_bytes) {
175:                report << "|" << b_micro << "|" << concurrent << "|" << rows_per_launch_group
176:                       << "|skip|skip|skip|skip|skip|skip|" << scratch_bytes
177:                       << ": estimated allocation exceeds available GPU memory"
178:                       << " free_bytes=" << free_bytes
179:                       << " io_bytes=" << io_bytes << "|\n";
180:                std::cout << "stream1_transformer_micro_skip"
181:                          << " b_micro=" << b_micro
182:                          << " concurrency=" << concurrent
183:                          << " scratch_bytes=" << scratch_bytes
184:                          << " free_bytes=" << free_bytes
185:                          << " reason=estimated_allocation_exceeds_available_memory\n";
186:                continue;
187:            }
188:
189:            Stream1TransformerBenchmarkResources resources;
190:            resources.create_streams_checked(concurrent);
191:            resources.allocate(model, b_micro, concurrent);
192:            std::vector<Stream1TransformerScratchView> scratch_views;
193:            scratch_views.reserve(concurrent);
194:            for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < concurrent; ++i) {
195:                scratch_views.push_back(stream1_weights::transformer_scratch_view(resources.scratch, model, b_micro, i));
196:                const std::uint64_t base = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(i) * b_micro;
197:                BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(resources.parent_base[i], &base, sizeof(base), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
198:                BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(resources.count[i], &b_micro, sizeof(b_micro), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
199:            }
200:
201:            const std::uint32_t iterations = b_micro >= 4096 ? 4U : 6U;
202:            float ms = 0.0f;
203:            if (graph_bench) {
204:                std::vector<cudaGraph_t> graphs(concurrent, nullptr);
205:                std::vector<cudaGraphExec_t> execs(concurrent, nullptr);
206:                for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < concurrent; ++i) {
207:                    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(resources.streams[i], cudaStreamCaptureModeGlobal));
208:                    stream1_transformer_inference_cuda(
209:                        states,
210:                        resources.parent_base[i],
211:                        resources.count[i],
212:                        view_holder.view,
213:                        scratch_views[i],
214:                        resources.score[i],
215:                        b_micro,
216:                        resources.streams[i]);
217:                    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(resources.streams[i], &graphs[i]));
218:                    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&execs[i], graphs[i], nullptr, nullptr, 0));
219:                }
220:                ms = time_transformer_graph_ms(resources.streams, iterations, execs);
221:                for (cudaGraphExec_t exec : execs) {
222:                    cudaGraphExecDestroy(exec);
223:                }
224:                for (cudaGraph_t graph : graphs) {
225:                    cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
226:                }
227:            } else {
228:                ms = time_gpu_ms(resources.streams, iterations, [&]() {
229:                    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < concurrent; ++i) {
230:                        stream1_transformer_inference_cuda(
231:                            states,
232:                            resources.parent_base[i],
233:                            resources.count[i],
234:                            view_holder.view,
235:                            scratch_views[i],
236:                            resources.score[i],
237:                            b_micro,
238:                            resources.streams[i]);
239:                    }
240:                });
241:            }
242:            const ScoreSummary score_summary = summarize_score_buffers(resources.score, b_micro, concurrent);
243:            const double parents = static_cast<double>(rows_per_launch_group);
244:            const double parent_per_sec = parents * 1000.0 / static_cast<double>(ms);
245:            const double candidate_per_sec = parents * static_cast<double>(MOVE_COUNT) * 1000.0 / static_cast<double>(ms);
246:            results.push_back(Stream1Result{b_micro, concurrent, ms, parent_per_sec, candidate_per_sec});
247:            report << "|" << b_micro
248:                   << "|" << concurrent
249:                   << "|" << rows_per_launch_group
250:                   << "|" << std::fixed << std::setprecision(4) << ms
251:                   << "|" << std::setprecision(1) << parent_per_sec
252:                   << "|" << candidate_per_sec
253:                   << "|" << score_summary.checksum
254:                   << "|" << score_summary.score_key_digest
255:                   << "|`" << score_summary.first_score_keys << "`"
256:                   << "|" << scratch_bytes << "|\n";
257:            std::cout << "stream1_transformer_micro"
258:                      << " b_micro=" << b_micro
259:                      << " concurrency=" << concurrent
260:                      << " rows_per_launch_group=" << rows_per_launch_group
261:                      << " ms_per_launch_group=" << std::fixed << std::setprecision(4) << ms
262:                      << " parents_per_sec=" << std::setprecision(1) << parent_per_sec
263:                      << " candidates_per_sec=" << candidate_per_sec
264:                      << " checksum=" << score_summary.checksum
265:                      << " score_key_digest=" << score_summary.score_key_digest
266:                      << " first_score_keys=" << score_summary.first_score_keys
267:                      << " scratch_bytes=" << scratch_bytes
268:                      << "\n";
269:        }
270:    }
271:    report << "\n";
272:    return results;
273:}
274:
275:
276:} // namespace beam::bench

86:inline std::uint64_t parse_u64(const char* text, const char* name) {
87:    char* end = nullptr;
88:    const unsigned long long value = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
89:    if (end == text || *end != '\0') {
90:        throw std::invalid_argument(std::string("invalid numeric argument: ") + name);
91:    }
92:    return static_cast<std::uint64_t>(value);
93:}
94:
95:inline std::uint32_t parse_next_u32(const std::string& text, std::size_t& pos, const char* context) {
96:    while (pos < text.size() && (text[pos] < '0' || text[pos] > '9')) {
97:        ++pos;
98:    }
99:    if (pos >= text.size()) {
100:        throw std::runtime_error(std::string("missing integer in ") + context);
101:    }
102:    std::uint64_t value = 0;
103:    while (pos < text.size() && text[pos] >= '0' && text[pos] <= '9') {
104:        value = value * 10ULL + static_cast<std::uint64_t>(text[pos] - '0');
105:        if (value > std::numeric_limits<std::uint8_t>::max()) {
106:            throw std::runtime_error(std::string("integer out of uint8 range in ") + context);
107:        }
108:        ++pos;
109:    }
110:    return static_cast<std::uint32_t>(value);
111:}
112:
113:inline std::string read_text_file(const std::filesystem::path& path) {
114:    std::ifstream file(path, std::ios::binary);
115:    if (!file) {
116:        throw std::runtime_error("cannot open required text file: " + path.string());
117:    }
118:    return std::string(std::istreambuf_iterator<char>(file), std::istreambuf_iterator<char>());
119:}
120:
121:inline std::vector<std::uint8_t> load_p900_generators(const std::filesystem::path& path) {
122:    const std::string text = read_text_file(path);
123:    std::size_t pos = text.find("\"actions\"");
124:    if (pos == std::string::npos) {
125:        throw std::runtime_error("p900 generator json missing actions");
126:    }
127:    pos = text.find('[', pos);
128:    if (pos == std::string::npos) {
129:        throw std::runtime_error("p900 generator json malformed actions");
130:    }
131:    std::vector<std::uint8_t> generators(MOVE_COUNT * STATE_STORAGE_LEN);
132:    for (std::uint32_t move = 0; move < MOVE_COUNT; ++move) {
133:        pos = text.find('[', pos + 1);
134:        if (pos == std::string::npos) {
135:            throw std::runtime_error("p900 generator json missing move array");
136:        }
137:        for (std::uint32_t p = 0; p < STATE_LEN; ++p) {
138:            generators[move * STATE_STORAGE_LEN + p] =
139:                static_cast<std::uint8_t>(parse_next_u32(text, pos, "p900 generator"));
140:        }
141:        for (std::uint32_t p = STATE_LEN; p < STATE_STORAGE_LEN; ++p) {
142:            generators[move * STATE_STORAGE_LEN + p] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(p);
143:        }
144:    }
145:    return generators;
146:}
147:
148:inline State128 load_central_state(const std::filesystem::path& path) {
149:    const std::string text = read_text_file(path);
150:    std::size_t pos = text.find("\"central_state\"");
151:    if (pos == std::string::npos) {
152:        throw std::runtime_error("puzzle info json missing central_state");
153:    }
154:    pos = text.find('[', pos);
155:    if (pos == std::string::npos) {
156:        throw std::runtime_error("puzzle info json malformed central_state");
157:    }
158:    State128 state{};
159:    for (std::uint32_t p = 0; p < STATE_LEN; ++p) {
160:        state.v[p] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(parse_next_u32(text, pos, "central_state"));
161:    }
162:    for (std::uint32_t p = STATE_LEN; p < STATE_STORAGE_LEN; ++p) {
163:        state.v[p] = 0;
164:    }
165:    return state;
166:}
167:
168:inline State128 load_initial_state_from_test_csv(const std::filesystem::path& path, std::uint64_t puzzle_id) {
169:    std::ifstream file(path);
170:    if (!file) {
171:        throw std::runtime_error("cannot open required csv file: " + path.string());
172:    }
173:    std::string line;
174:    std::getline(file, line);
175:    while (std::getline(file, line)) {
176:        const std::size_t comma = line.find(',');
177:        if (comma == std::string::npos) {
178:            continue;
179:        }
180:        const std::uint64_t row_id = parse_u64(line.substr(0, comma).c_str(), "initial_state_id");
181:        if (row_id != puzzle_id) {
182:            continue;
183:        }
184:        const std::size_t first_quote = line.find('"', comma);
185:        const std::size_t last_quote = line.rfind('"');
186:        if (first_quote == std::string::npos || last_quote == std::string::npos || last_quote <= first_quote) {
187:            throw std::runtime_error("test csv malformed initial_state row");
188:        }
189:        const std::string state_text = line.substr(first_quote + 1, last_quote - first_quote - 1);
190:        std::size_t pos = 0;
191:        State128 state{};
192:        for (std::uint32_t p = 0; p < STATE_LEN; ++p) {
193:            state.v[p] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(parse_next_u32(state_text, pos, "initial_state"));
194:        }
195:        for (std::uint32_t p = STATE_LEN; p < STATE_STORAGE_LEN; ++p) {
196:            state.v[p] = 0;
197:        }
198:        return state;
199:    }
200:    throw std::runtime_error("requested puzzle_id not found in test csv");
201:}
202:
203:inline void require_aligned(const void* ptr, std::uintptr_t alignment, const char* name) {
204:    if (reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(ptr) % alignment != 0) {
205:        throw std::runtime_error(std::string("device pointer alignment failed: ") + name);
206:    }
207:}

209:template <typename T>
210:T* device_alloc(std::uint64_t count) {
211:    T* ptr = nullptr;
212:    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&ptr, count * sizeof(T)));
213:    return ptr;
214:}
215:
216:struct Timer {
217:    cudaStream_t control = nullptr;
218:    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
219:    cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
220:    std::vector<cudaEvent_t> done;
221:
222:    explicit Timer(std::uint32_t stream_count) {
223:        BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&control, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
224:        BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
225:        BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
226:        done.resize(stream_count);
227:        for (cudaEvent_t& event : done) {
228:            BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&event));
229:        }
230:    }
231:
232:    ~Timer() {
233:        for (cudaEvent_t event : done) {
234:            cudaEventDestroy(event);
235:        }
236:        cudaEventDestroy(start);
237:        cudaEventDestroy(stop);
238:        cudaStreamDestroy(control);
239:    }
240:};
241:
242:template <typename Fn>
243:float time_gpu_ms(const std::vector<cudaStream_t>& streams, std::uint32_t iterations, Fn launch) {
244:    Timer timer(static_cast<std::uint32_t>(streams.size()));
245:    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
246:    launch();
247:    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
248:    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(timer.start, timer.control));
249:    for (cudaStream_t stream : streams) {
250:        BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(stream, timer.start, 0));
251:    }
252:    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < iterations; ++i) {
253:        launch();
254:    }
255:    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < streams.size(); ++i) {
256:        BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(timer.done[i], streams[i]));
257:        BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(timer.control, timer.done[i], 0));
258:    }
259:    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(timer.stop, timer.control));
260:    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(timer.stop));
261:    float elapsed_ms = 0.0f;
262:    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsed_ms, timer.start, timer.stop));
263:    return elapsed_ms / static_cast<float>(iterations);
264:}
265:
266:template <typename Fn>
267:float time_single_stream_ms(cudaStream_t stream, Fn launch) {
268:    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
269:    cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
270:    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
271:    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
272:    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
273:    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start, stream));
274:    launch();
275:    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop, stream));
276:    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
277:    float elapsed_ms = 0.0f;
278:    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsed_ms, start, stop));
279:    cudaEventDestroy(start);
280:    cudaEventDestroy(stop);
281:    return elapsed_ms;
282:}
283:
284:inline std::vector<cudaStream_t> create_streams(std::uint32_t count) {
285:    std::vector<cudaStream_t> streams(count);
286:    for (cudaStream_t& stream : streams) {
287:        BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
288:    }
289:    return streams;
290:}
291:
292:inline void destroy_streams(std::vector<cudaStream_t>& streams) {
293:    for (cudaStream_t stream : streams) {
294:        cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
295:    }
296:    streams.clear();
297:}

299:struct Stream1Result {
300:    std::uint32_t b_micro = 0;
301:    std::uint32_t concurrent = 0;
302:    float ms = 0.0f;
303:    double parent_per_sec = 0.0;
304:    double candidate_per_sec = 0.0;
305:};
306:
307:struct StreamResult {
308:    std::string name;
309:    std::uint32_t param_a = 0;
310:    std::uint32_t param_b = 0;
311:    float ms = 0.0f;
312:    double candidate_per_sec = 0.0;
313:};
314:
315:inline std::vector<State128> make_state_batch(
316:    State128 seed,
317:    std::uint32_t count,
318:    std::uint32_t num_classes) {
319:    std::vector<State128> states(count, seed);
320:    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
321:        states[i].v[0] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>((states[i].v[0] + i) % num_classes);
322:        states[i].v[STATE_LEN] = 0;
323:    }
324:    return states;
325:}
326:
327:inline std::vector<State128> make_synthetic_state_batch(
328:    std::uint32_t count,
329:    std::uint32_t state_len,
330:    std::uint32_t num_classes) {
331:    if (state_len > STATE_LEN) {
332:        throw std::runtime_error("synthetic state_len exceeds logical State128 bytes");
333:    }
334:    std::vector<State128> states(count);
335:    for (std::uint32_t row = 0; row < count; ++row) {
336:        for (std::uint32_t p = 0; p < STATE_STORAGE_LEN; ++p) {
337:            states[row].v[p] = 0;
338:        }
339:        for (std::uint32_t p = 0; p < state_len; ++p) {
340:            const std::uint64_t value =
341:                (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(row) * state_len + p) * 17ULL + 23ULL;
342:            states[row].v[p] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(value % num_classes);
343:        }
344:    }
345:    return states;
346:}

1:# Transformer Inference Architecture Notes
2:
3:Date: 2026-06-30
4:Branch: `codex/stream1-piece-transformer`
5:
6:## Direction
7:
8:User direction is explicit: no distillation into MLP and no fallback paths. This work keeps the Stream1 `piece_transformer` backend as a real transformer backend and optimizes it directly.
9:
10:## Current Model
11:
12:The current Kaggle p900 `piece_transformer` backend is correct but not yet optimized like the existing MLP backend.
13:
14:Current manifest shape:
15:
16:- `state_len=120`
17:- `num_pieces=50`
18:- `seq_len=51` including CLS
19:- `d_model=256`
20:- `nhead=8`
21:- `head_dim=32`
22:- `transformer_layers=4`
23:- `ff_dim=1024`
24:- `output_dim=24`
25:- CLS pooling
26:- SiLU FFN
27:
28:One parent state becomes 51 token rows. Each layer runs LayerNorm, QKV projection, attention, attention output projection, another LayerNorm, FFN up/down projections, SiLU, residual adds, then final CLS LayerNorm and output projection.
29:
30:## Current Backend Optimization Path
31:
32:The immediate route is to make this exact transformer backend production-grade:
33:
34:1. Add a transformer Stream1 microbenchmark.
35:2. Tune transformer-specific `B_MICRO`, concurrency, ring slots, and shard sizing separately from MLP.
36:3. Remove redundant transformer scratch clears.
37:4. Remove global `attention_scores_probs` scratch and compute softmax/value accumulation in shared/register storage per `(row, head, query)`.
38:5. Fuse or combine simple epilogues where safe: bias, SiLU, residual add, score quantization.
39:6. Keep all steady-state allocations preallocated and graph-capturable.
40:
41:This preserves the trained model and avoids fallback behavior.
42:
43:## Smaller Transformer Variants
44:
45:A smaller transformer such as 2 layers, `d_model=128..192`, and `ff_dim=512..768` could reduce GEMM/scratch cost while staying a transformer backend. It requires relaxing current exporter/runtime shape checks, which currently reject non-4-layer/non-1024-FF models.
46:
47:Risk: medium quality loss. Runtime friendliness: medium. This is valid only as an explicit transformer backend variant, not a fallback to MLP.
48:
49:## Piece-Mixer As Transformer-Like Backend
50:
51:A piece-mixer keeps fixed piece tokens but replaces content-dependent QKV attention with deterministic token mixing and channel mixing:
52:
53:```text
54:piece embedding -> channel projection -> repeated [token-mix over pieces, channel-mix over channels] -> pool -> 24 scores
55:```
56:
57:This is not an MLP fallback. It would be a new explicit piece-token backend with transformer-like token processing but no softmax attention. It is mostly GEMM plus fused bias/activation/residual kernels and removes attention score/probability scratch.
58:
59:Risk: medium. Runtime friendliness: high. Engineering cost: medium/high because it needs a new manifest/export/runtime path.
60:
61:## Transformer Reranking
62:
63:A transformer-only reranking stage could be considered later, but not as a fallback. It would need explicit Stream1 semantics: score all required candidates or define a formally safe two-stage scoring contract before Stream2/3/4 consume score keys.
64:
65:This is invasive and should not be the first optimization.
66:
67:## Recommendation
68:
69:Do current-backend optimization first: benchmark, remove redundant clears, remove global attention scratch, tune transformer-specific batching/concurrency, and then consider smaller transformer or piece-mixer variants. Do not train or wire an MLP distillation path for this track.
70:## 2026-07-04 Backend Direction Update
71:
72:After the native CUDA/CUTLASS v19 extended 2xT4 sweep, the best verified native aggregate is `1224735.7` candidates/s, about `0.971x` of the PyTorch `batch_process` reference aggregate. The native experiments remain archived under `docs/archive/stream1_transformer_native/`.
73:
74:The transformer direction is now three explicit execution backends for the same `piece_transformer` model family: `pytorch`, `libtorch`, and `native_cutlass`. This does not change the manifest model backend, does not distill to MLP, and does not add fallback behavior. Default builds remain Torch-free; the C++ LibTorch benchmark/tool target is enabled only with `BEAM_ENABLE_LIBTORCH_STREAM1=ON`.
75:
76:Production dispatcher integration remains a separate step for non-native paths because the current dispatcher captures Stream1+Stream2 CUDA Graph templates. LibTorch op dispatch must either be proven graph-capture safe with stable allocations or run through a named non-graph Stream1 execution path. The PyTorch path stays a Python/Kaggle execution backend unless an explicit bridge is designed.
77:## 2026-07-04 CUTLASS Profiling Update
78:
79:A later Docker Nsight pass compared the explicit C++ LibTorch/cuBLAS path against the native CUTLASS Stream1 transformer path on local RTX 3070. The native CUTLASS backend is already the practical cuBLAS replacement route for production: local b384/c1 reached `871235.6` candidates/s and b512/c2 reached `991974.7`, versus local LibTorch eager `578741`. Nsight Compute 2025.1.1 confirmed both cuBLASLt and CUTLASS sampled GEMMs use HMMA tensor cores.
80:
81:The current bottleneck is not missing tensor-core use. Native CUTLASS sampled kernels are around `40-43%` SM throughput and `62-70%` DRAM throughput, so the next work should tune transformer-specific GEMM/epilogue/layout/LayerNorm structure. A wider `128x128x32` QKV/FF1 tile regressed versus the current `128x64x32` tile and was reverted. Evidence: `test_results/stream1_transformer_cutlass_vs_libtorch_profile_2026-07-04.md`.
82:## 2026-07-04 Three-Backend Policy
83:
84:The backend registry is `tools/stream1_transformer_backends.py`; details are in `docs/stream1_transformer_backends.md`.
85:
86:The three supported backend names are:
87:
88:- `pytorch`: Python/Torch execution, including the Kaggle notebook path and exported-weight Torch benchmark.
89:- `libtorch`: explicit C++ LibTorch execution over exported weights.
90:- `native_cutlass`: native C++/CUDA/CUTLASS execution through the existing Stream1 transformer runtime.
91:
92:The selection is explicit. None of these paths may silently fall back to another path. They should be optimized and verified independently.
93:## 2026-07-05 Backend Parity Contract
94:
95:The three Stream1 `piece_transformer` backends now share a parity launcher contract. PyTorch and LibTorch benchmarks emit `checksum` and `first_score_keys`, and the native CUTLASS transformer microbenchmark emits the same fields while optionally using `BEAM_STREAM1_SYNTHETIC_STATES=1` for deterministic synthetic inputs.
96:
97:`tools/stream1_transformer_parity.py` is the correctness gate for comparing explicit backends on one synthetic batch. It supports a dry-run mode for clean checkout validation and an execution mode for GPU hosts with exported weights and built backend binaries. This is only a verification layer; production dispatch still selects one backend explicitly and must not silently fall back.
98:## 2026-07-05 Score-Key Digest Gate
99:
100:The backend parity contract now includes `score_key_digest`, a deterministic FNV-1a 64-bit digest over all quantized score keys in batch-major, move-major order. The digest is emitted by the PyTorch benchmark, the C++ LibTorch benchmark, and the native CUTLASS transformer microbenchmark. Parity execution should treat digest mismatch as a hard correctness failure; checksum and first-row score keys are secondary diagnostics.

````

712:    }

715:void stream1_transformer_linear_residual_cuda(
716:    const half* input,
717:    const half* weight,
718:    half* residual_inout,
719:    std::uint32_t rows,
720:    std::uint32_t input_cols,
721:    std::uint32_t output_cols,
722:    std::uint32_t dtype,
723:    cudaStream_t stream) {
724:    if (dtype == STREAM1_DTYPE_BF16) {
725:        int device = 0;
726:        cudaDeviceProp prop{};
727:        BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
728:        BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&prop, device));
729:        if (prop.major < 8) {
730:            throw std::invalid_argument("Stream1 piece_transformer bf16 residual GEMM requires SM80+");
731:        }
732:        stream1_transformer_linear_residual_typed<
733:            cutlass::bfloat16_t,
