```text
748:template <typename Element, typename ArchTag, typename InstructionShape>
749:void stream1_transformer_linear_bias_typed(
750:    const half* input,
751:    const half* weight,
752:    const half* bias,
753:    half* output,
754:    std::uint32_t rows,
755:    std::uint32_t input_cols,
756:    std::uint32_t output_cols,
757:    cudaStream_t stream) {
758:    constexpr int elements_per_access = 128 / cutlass::sizeof_bits<Element>::value;
759:    using Epilogue = cutlass::epilogue::thread::LinearCombinationBiasElementwise<
760:        Element,
761:        float,
762:        float,
763:        Element,
764:        Element,
765:        elements_per_access,
766:        cutlass::epilogue::thread::Identity<float>,
767:        cutlass::plus<float>,
768:        false,
769:        Element>;
770:    using Gemm = cutlass::gemm::device::GemmUniversalWithBroadcast<
771:        Element,
772:        cutlass::layout::RowMajor,
773:        Element,
774:        cutlass::layout::RowMajor,
775:        Element,
776:        cutlass::layout::RowMajor,
777:        float,
778:        cutlass::arch::OpClassTensorOp,
779:        ArchTag,
780:        cutlass::gemm::GemmShape<128, 64, 32>,
781:        cutlass::gemm::GemmShape<64, 32, 32>,
782:        InstructionShape,
783:        Epilogue>;
784:
785:    const cutlass::gemm::GemmCoord problem(
786:        static_cast<int>(rows),
787:        static_cast<int>(output_cols),
788:        static_cast<int>(input_cols));
789:    typename Epilogue::Params epilogue_params{1.0f, 0.0f};
790:    const std::int64_t batch_stride_a = static_cast<std::int64_t>(rows) * input_cols;
791:    const std::int64_t batch_stride_b = static_cast<std::int64_t>(input_cols) * output_cols;
792:    const std::int64_t batch_stride_output = static_cast<std::int64_t>(rows) * output_cols;
793:
794:    typename Gemm::Arguments args(
795:        cutlass::gemm::GemmUniversalMode::kGemm,
796:        problem,
797:        1,
798:        epilogue_params,
799:        reinterpret_cast<Element const*>(input),
800:        reinterpret_cast<Element const*>(weight),
801:        reinterpret_cast<Element const*>(output),
802:        reinterpret_cast<Element*>(output),
803:        const_cast<Element*>(reinterpret_cast<Element const*>(bias)),
804:        nullptr,
805:        batch_stride_a,
806:        batch_stride_b,
807:        batch_stride_output,
808:        batch_stride_output,
809:        0,
810:        0,
811:        static_cast<int>(input_cols),
812:        static_cast<int>(output_cols),
813:        static_cast<int>(output_cols),
814:        static_cast<int>(output_cols),
815:        0,
816:        static_cast<int>(output_cols));
817:
818:    Gemm gemm;
819:    const cutlass::Status status = gemm(args, nullptr, stream);
820:    if (status != cutlass::Status::kSuccess) {
821:        throw std::runtime_error("Stream1 piece_transformer linear+bias GEMM launch failed");
822:    }
823:}

969:template <typename Element, typename ArchTag, typename InstructionShape>
970:void stream1_transformer_qk_batched_launch_typed(
971:    const half* qkv,
972:    half* scores_probs,
973:    std::uint32_t b_micro,
974:    cudaStream_t stream) {
975:    const cutlass::gemm::GemmCoord problem(
976:        static_cast<int>(STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_SEQ51),
977:        static_cast<int>(STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_SEQ51),
978:        static_cast<int>(STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_HEAD_DIM32));
979:    constexpr float scale = 0.1767766952966369f;
980:    const std::int64_t qkv_batch_stride = static_cast<std::int64_t>(
981:        STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_SEQ51 * STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_QKV_STRIDE51);
982:    const std::int64_t score_batch_stride = static_cast<std::int64_t>(STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_SCORE_STRIDE51);
983:    cutlass::Status status = cutlass::Status::kSuccess;
984:    for (std::uint32_t head = 0; head < STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_NHEAD8; ++head) {
985:        const std::uint64_t head_offset = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(head) * STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_HEAD_DIM32;
986:        const half* q_base = qkv + head_offset;
987:        const half* k_base = qkv + STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_DMODEL256 + head_offset;
988:        half* score_base = scores_probs +
989:            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(head) * b_micro * STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_SCORE_STRIDE51;
990:
991:        using Gemm = cutlass::gemm::device::GemmBatched<
992:            Element,
993:            cutlass::layout::RowMajor,
994:            Element,
995:            cutlass::layout::ColumnMajor,
996:            Element,
997:            cutlass::layout::RowMajor,
998:            float,
999:            cutlass::arch::OpClassTensorOp,
1000:            ArchTag,
1001:            cutlass::gemm::GemmShape<64, 64, 32>,
1002:            cutlass::gemm::GemmShape<32, 32, 32>,
1003:            InstructionShape>;
1004:        Gemm gemm;
1005:        typename Gemm::Arguments args{
1006:            problem,
1007:            {reinterpret_cast<Element const*>(q_base), static_cast<int>(STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_QKV_STRIDE51)},
1008:            qkv_batch_stride,
1009:            {reinterpret_cast<Element const*>(k_base), static_cast<int>(STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_QKV_STRIDE51)},
1010:            qkv_batch_stride,
1011:            {reinterpret_cast<Element const*>(score_base), static_cast<int>(STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_SCORE_ROW_STRIDE51)},
1012:            score_batch_stride,
1013:            {reinterpret_cast<Element*>(score_base), static_cast<int>(STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_SCORE_ROW_STRIDE51)},
1014:            score_batch_stride,
1015:            {scale, 0.0f},
1016:            static_cast<int>(b_micro)};
1017:        status = gemm(args, nullptr, stream);
1018:        if (status != cutlass::Status::kSuccess) {
1019:            throw std::runtime_error("Stream1 piece_transformer QK GemmBatched launch failed");
1020:        }
1021:    }
1022:}

1024:template <typename Element, typename ArchTag, typename InstructionShape>
1025:void stream1_transformer_pv_batched_launch_typed(
1026:    const half* qkv,
1027:    half* scores_probs,
1028:    half* context,
1029:    std::uint32_t b_micro,
1030:    cudaStream_t stream) {
1031:    const cutlass::gemm::GemmCoord problem(
1032:        static_cast<int>(STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_SEQ51),
1033:        static_cast<int>(STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_HEAD_DIM32),
1034:        static_cast<int>(STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_SCORE_ROW_STRIDE51));
1035:    const std::int64_t score_batch_stride = static_cast<std::int64_t>(STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_SCORE_STRIDE51);
1036:    const std::int64_t context_batch_stride = static_cast<std::int64_t>(
1037:        STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_SEQ51 * STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_DMODEL256);
1038:    cutlass::Status status = cutlass::Status::kSuccess;
1039:    for (std::uint32_t head = 0; head < STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_NHEAD8; ++head) {
1040:        const std::uint64_t head_offset = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(head) * STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_HEAD_DIM32;
1041:        const half* prob_base = scores_probs +
1042:            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(head) * b_micro * STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_SCORE_STRIDE51;
1043:        const half* value_base = prob_base + STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_PROB_STRIDE51;
1044:        half* context_base = context + head_offset;
1045:
1046:        using Gemm = cutlass::gemm::device::GemmBatched<
1047:            Element,
1048:            cutlass::layout::RowMajor,
1049:            Element,
1050:            cutlass::layout::RowMajor,
1051:            Element,
1052:            cutlass::layout::RowMajor,
1053:            float,
1054:            cutlass::arch::OpClassTensorOp,
1055:            ArchTag,
1056:            cutlass::gemm::GemmShape<64, 32, 32>,
1057:            cutlass::gemm::GemmShape<32, 32, 32>,
1058:            InstructionShape>;
1059:        Gemm gemm;
1060:        typename Gemm::Arguments args{
1061:            problem,
1062:            {reinterpret_cast<Element const*>(prob_base), static_cast<int>(STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_SCORE_ROW_STRIDE51)},
1063:            score_batch_stride,
1064:            {reinterpret_cast<Element const*>(value_base), static_cast<int>(STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_HEAD_DIM32)},
1065:            score_batch_stride,
1066:            {reinterpret_cast<Element const*>(context_base), static_cast<int>(STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_DMODEL256)},
1067:            context_batch_stride,
1068:            {reinterpret_cast<Element*>(context_base), static_cast<int>(STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_DMODEL256)},
1069:            context_batch_stride,
1070:            {1.0f, 0.0f},
1071:            static_cast<int>(b_micro)};
1072:        status = gemm(args, nullptr, stream);
1073:        if (status != cutlass::Status::kSuccess) {
1074:            throw std::runtime_error("Stream1 piece_transformer PV GemmBatched launch failed");
1075:        }
1076:    }
1077:}
```

```text
165:inline std::string read_text_exact(const std::filesystem::path& path) {

167:    if (!file) {
168:        throw std::runtime_error("cannot open required text file: " + path.string());

171:}
172:
173:inline std::vector<std::byte> read_binary_exact(
174:    const std::filesystem::path& path,
175:    std::uint64_t expected_bytes) {
176:    if (expected_bytes > static_cast<std::uint64_t>(std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max())) {
177:        throw std::runtime_error("binary expected size exceeds size_t: " + path.string());
178:    }
179:    std::ifstream file(path, std::ios::binary);
180:    if (!file) {
181:        throw std::runtime_error("cannot open required binary file: " + path.string());
182:    }
183:    std::vector<std::byte> bytes(static_cast<std::size_t>(expected_bytes));
184:    file.read(reinterpret_cast<char*>(bytes.data()), static_cast<std::streamsize>(bytes.size()));
185:    const std::size_t actual = static_cast<std::size_t>(file.gcount());
186:    file.peek();
187:    if (actual != bytes.size() || !file.eof()) {
188:        throw std::runtime_error(
189:            "binary file size mismatch: " + path.string() +
190:            " expected=" + std::to_string(bytes.size()) +
191:            " actual_read=" + std::to_string(actual));
192:    }
193:    return bytes;
194:}
195:
196:inline bool manifest_has_key(const std::string& text, const char* key) {
197:    const std::string quoted = std::string("\"") + key + "\"";
198:    return text.find(quoted) != std::string::npos;
199:}
200:
201:inline std::uint32_t parse_manifest_u32(const std::string& text, const char* key) {
202:    const std::string quoted = std::string("\"") + key + "\"";
203:    std::size_t pos = text.find(quoted);
204:    if (pos == std::string::npos) {
205:        throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest missing key: " + std::string(key));
206:    }
207:    pos = text.find(':', pos + quoted.size());
208:    if (pos == std::string::npos) {
209:        throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest malformed key: " + std::string(key));
210:    }
211:    ++pos;
212:    while (pos < text.size() && (text[pos] == ' ' || text[pos] == '\t' || text[pos] == '\r' || text[pos] == '\n')) {
213:        ++pos;
214:    }
215:    std::uint64_t value = 0;
216:    std::size_t digits = 0;
217:    while (pos < text.size() && text[pos] >= '0' && text[pos] <= '9') {
218:        value = value * 10ULL + static_cast<std::uint64_t>(text[pos] - '0');
219:        if (value > std::numeric_limits<std::uint32_t>::max()) {
220:            throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest key exceeds uint32: " + std::string(key));
221:        }
222:        ++pos;
223:        ++digits;
224:    }
225:    if (digits == 0) {
226:        throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest key is not uint32: " + std::string(key));
227:    }
228:    return static_cast<std::uint32_t>(value);
229:}
230:
231:inline std::uint32_t parse_manifest_u32_any(
232:    const std::string& text,
233:    const char* primary_key,
234:    const char* fallback_key) {
235:    const bool primary_present = manifest_has_key(text, primary_key);
236:    const bool fallback_present = manifest_has_key(text, fallback_key);
237:    if (!primary_present) {
238:        return parse_manifest_u32(text, fallback_key);
239:    }
240:    const std::uint32_t primary = parse_manifest_u32(text, primary_key);
241:    if (!fallback_present) {
242:        return primary;
243:    }
244:    const std::uint32_t fallback = parse_manifest_u32(text, fallback_key);
245:    if (primary != fallback) {
246:        throw std::runtime_error(
247:            "stream1 manifest conflicting uint32 keys: " + std::string(primary_key) +
248:            "=" + std::to_string(primary) + " " + std::string(fallback_key) +
249:            "=" + std::to_string(fallback));
250:    }
251:    return primary;
252:}
253:

258:        throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest missing key: " + std::string(key));
259:    }
```

```text
580:inline std::uint64_t transformer_scratch_bytes_for_rows(const Stream1ModelConfig& model, std::uint64_t rows) {
581:    const std::uint64_t token_values = rows * model.seq_len * model.d_model;
582:    const std::uint64_t qkv_values = rows * model.seq_len * 3ULL * model.d_model;
583:    const std::uint64_t score_values = rows * model.nhead * transformer_attention_score_stride(model);
584:    const std::uint64_t context_values = rows * model.seq_len * model.d_model;
585:    const std::uint64_t ff_values = rows * model.seq_len * model.ff_dim;
586:    const std::uint64_t logits_values = rows * model.output_dim;
587:    return fp16_bytes(token_values + qkv_values + score_values + context_values + ff_values + logits_values);
588:}
589:
590:inline std::uint64_t stream1_scratch_bytes(
591:    const Stream1ModelConfig& model,
592:    std::uint32_t b_micro,
593:    std::uint32_t lane_count) {
594:    const std::uint64_t rows = stream1_inference_rows(b_micro, model) * lane_count;
595:    switch (model.backend) {
596:    case STREAM1_BACKEND_MLP:
597:        return fp16_bytes(rows * (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(model.hidden1) + 2ULL * model.hidden2 + model.output_dim));
598:    case STREAM1_BACKEND_PIECE_TRANSFORMER:
599:        return transformer_scratch_bytes_for_rows(model, rows);
600:    default:
601:        throw std::runtime_error("unsupported Stream1 backend in scratch byte helper");
602:    }
```

````text
 ```bash

   -DBEAM_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES=75
 cmake --build build-transformer --target stream_benchmark -j2
 ```
 
 ## Kaggle 2xT4
 
 The private Kaggle package `kaggle_t4_transformer_backend_compare/` currently runs all three families in one job:
 
 - original PyTorch `batch_process`;
 - Python Torch over exported weights;
 - C++ LibTorch eager/CUDA Graph;
 - native CUTLASS eager/graph.
 
 For strategy decisions, use the aggregate backend summary from that package. For production integration work, keep the three backend families independently selectable through the launcher above.
 ## Parity Gate
 
-Use `tools/stream1_transformer_parity.py` to compare selected backends on the same synthetic state batch. This gate is explicit backend comparison, not runtime fallback behavior.
+Use `tools/stream1_transformer_parity.py` to compare selected backends on the same synthetic state batch. This gate is explicit backend comparison, not runtime fallback behavior. Real runs require a matching `score_key_digest`, a stable FNV-1a 64-bit digest over every quantized score key in batch-major, move-major order; `first_score_keys` remains a small readable sanity check.
 
 Dry-run the parity plan without requiring weights or built binaries:
 
 ```bash
 python tools/stream1_transformer_parity.py \
   --weight-dir /path/to/stream1_transformer_weights_fp16 \
   --build-dir build-transformer \
   --backends pytorch,libtorch,native_cutlass \
   --batch 256 \
   --dry-run
 ```
 
 Run the parity gate after the three backend tools are available:
 
 ```bash
 python tools/stream1_transformer_parity.py \
   --weight-dir /path/to/stream1_transformer_weights_fp16 \
   --build-dir build-transformer \
   --backends pytorch,libtorch,native_cutlass \
   --batch 256 \
diff --git a/docs/transformer_inference_architecture.md b/docs/transformer_inference_architecture.md
index 8315e66..c28deb3 100644
--- a/docs/transformer_inference_architecture.md
+++ b/docs/transformer_inference_architecture.md
@@ -78,20 +78,23 @@ Production dispatcher integration remains a separate step for non-native paths b
 
 A later Docker Nsight pass compared the explicit C++ LibTorch/cuBLAS path against the native CUTLASS Stream1 transformer path on local RTX 3070. The native CUTLASS backend is already the practical cuBLAS replacement route for production: local b384/c1 reached `871235.6` candidates/s and b512/c2 reached `991974.7`, versus local LibTorch eager `578741`. Nsight Compute 2025.1.1 confirmed both cuBLASLt and CUTLASS sampled GEMMs use HMMA tensor cores.
 
 The current bottleneck is not missing tensor-core use. Native CUTLASS sampled kernels are around `40-43%` SM throughput and `62-70%` DRAM throughput, so the next work should tune transformer-specific GEMM/epilogue/layout/LayerNorm structure. A wider `128x128x32` QKV/FF1 tile regressed versus the current `128x64x32` tile and was reverted. Evidence: `test_results/stream1_transformer_cutlass_vs_libtorch_profile_2026-07-04.md`.
 ## 2026-07-04 Three-Backend Policy
 
 The backend registry is `tools/stream1_transformer_backends.py`; details are in `docs/stream1_transformer_backends.md`.
 
 The three supported backend names are:
 
 - `pytorch`: Python/Torch execution, including the Kaggle notebook path and exported-weight Torch benchmark.
 - `libtorch`: explicit C++ LibTorch execution over exported weights.
 - `native_cutlass`: native C++/CUDA/CUTLASS execution through the existing Stream1 transformer runtime.
 
 The selection is explicit. None of these paths may silently fall back to another path. They should be optimized and verified independently.
 ## 2026-07-05 Backend Parity Contract
 
 The three Stream1 `piece_transformer` backends now share a parity launcher contract. PyTorch and LibTorch benchmarks emit `checksum` and `first_score_keys`, and the native CUTLASS transformer microbenchmark emits the same fields while optionally using `BEAM_STREAM1_SYNTHETIC_STATES=1` for deterministic synthetic inputs.
 
 `tools/stream1_transformer_parity.py` is the correctness gate for comparing explicit backends on one synthetic batch. It supports a dry-run mode for clean checkout validation and an execution mode for GPU hosts with exported weights and built backend binaries. This is only a verification layer; production dispatch still selects one backend explicitly and must not silently fall back.
+## 2026-07-05 Score-Key Digest Gate
+
+The backend parity contract now includes `score_key_digest`, a deterministic FNV-1a 64-bit digest over all quantized score keys in batch-major, move-major order. The digest is emitted by the PyTorch benchmark, the C++ LibTorch benchmark, and the native CUTLASS transformer microbenchmark. Parity execution should treat digest mismatch as a hard correctness failure; checksum and first-row score keys are secondary diagnostics.
diff --git a/tests/test_stream1_transformer_parity.py b/tests/test_stream1_transformer_parity.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..1ce5eed
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_stream1_transformer_parity.py
@@ -0,0 +1,74 @@
+import sys
+import unittest
+from pathlib import Path
+
+REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+if str(REPO_ROOT) not in sys.path:
+    sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO_ROOT))
+
+from tools.stream1_transformer_parity import BackendRun, compare_runs, parse_pairs
+
+
+def run(
+    backend: str,
+    *,
+    checksum: int = 100,
+    digest: int | None = 123456789,
+    first_score_keys: list[int] | None = None,
+    status: str = "ok",
+) -> BackendRun:
+    return BackendRun(
+        backend=backend,
+        mode="eager",
+        command=[backend],
+        env={},
+        return_code=0,
+        checksum=checksum,
+        score_key_digest=digest,
+        first_score_keys=first_score_keys if first_score_keys is not None else [10, 20, 30],
+        log_path="",
+        status=status,
+    )
+
+
+class Stream1TransformerParityTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_parse_pairs_reads_score_key_digest(self) -> None:
+        parsed = parse_pairs(
+            "stream1_transformer_micro checksum=42 score_key_digest=987 first_score_keys=1,2,3"
+        )
+        self.assertEqual(parsed["checksum"], "42")
+        self.assertEqual(parsed["score_key_digest"], "987")
+        self.assertEqual(parsed["first_score_keys"], "1,2,3")
+
+    def test_compare_passes_matching_digest_with_first_row_tolerance(self) -> None:
+        result = compare_runs(
+            [
+                run("pytorch", first_score_keys=[100, 200]),
+                run("libtorch", first_score_keys=[101, 199]),
+            ],
+            tolerance=2,
+        )
+        self.assertEqual(result["status"], "pass")
+
+    def test_compare_fails_digest_mismatch_even_when_row_matches(self) -> None:
+        result = compare_runs(
+            [
+                run("pytorch", checksum=100, digest=111, first_score_keys=[1, 2, 3]),
+                run("native_cutlass", checksum=100, digest=222, first_score_keys=[1, 2, 3]),
+            ],
+            tolerance=0,
+        )
+        self.assertEqual(result["status"], "failed")
+        self.assertEqual(result["comparisons"][1]["reason"], "score key digest mismatch")
+
+    def test_compare_fails_missing_digest(self) -> None:
+        result = compare_runs(
+            [run("pytorch", digest=111), run("libtorch", digest=None)],
+            tolerance=0,
+        )
+        self.assertEqual(result["status"], "failed")
+        self.assertEqual(result["comparisons"][1]["reason"], "missing score key digest")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()

+constexpr std::uint64_t FNV64_OFFSET = 1469598103934665603ULL;
+constexpr std::uint64_t FNV64_PRIME = 1099511628211ULL;
+
 struct Args {
     fs::path weight_dir;
     std::vector<std::int64_t> batches{384, 512, 768, 1024};
     std::int64_t warmup = 10;

 };
 
 Args parse_args(int argc, char** argv) {
     Args args;
     for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
         const std::string key = argv[i];
         auto require_value = [&](const char* name) -> std::string {
             if (i + 1 >= argc) {
                 throw std::runtime_error(std::string("missing value for ") + name);
             }
             return argv[++i];
         };
@@ -72,40 +75,55 @@ Args parse_args(int argc, char** argv) {
     }
     return args;
 }
 
 
 std::string format_first_score_keys(const torch::Tensor& keys) {
     if (keys.size(0) <= 0) {
         return "";
     }
     torch::Tensor first = keys.index({0}).to(torch::kCPU).contiguous();
     auto accessor = first.accessor<std::int64_t, 1>();
     std::ostringstream out;
     for (std::int64_t i = 0; i < first.size(0); ++i) {
         if (i != 0) {
             out << ',';
         }
         out << accessor[i];
     }
     return out.str();
 }
+
+std::uint64_t score_key_digest(const torch::Tensor& keys) {
+    torch::Tensor flat = keys.to(torch::kCPU).to(torch::kInt64).contiguous().view({-1});
+    auto accessor = flat.accessor<std::int64_t, 1>();
+    std::uint64_t digest = FNV64_OFFSET;
+    for (std::int64_t i = 0; i < flat.size(0); ++i) {
+        const std::uint32_t value = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(accessor[i]);
+        for (int shift = 0; shift < 32; shift += 8) {
+            digest ^= static_cast<std::uint64_t>((value >> shift) & 0xFFU);
+            digest *= FNV64_PRIME;
+        }
+    }
+    return digest;
+}
+
 std::int16_t cuda_device_index(const torch::Device& device) {
     if (!device.is_cuda()) {
         throw std::runtime_error("CUDA graph mode requires a CUDA device");
     }
     const std::int16_t index = device.index();
     return index >= 0 ? index : 0;
 }
 
 torch::Tensor run_eager(
     const beam::stream1_libtorch::PieceTransformerLibTorch& model,
     const torch::Tensor& states,
     std::int64_t warmup,

     double& elapsed_ms) {
     torch::Tensor logits;
     for (std::int64_t i = 0; i < warmup; ++i) {
         logits = model.forward(states);
     }
     beam::stream1_libtorch::synchronize_if_cuda(device);
@@ -188,48 +206,50 @@ int main(int argc, char** argv) {
                   << " device=" << device.str()
                   << " dtype=" << model.dtype_suffix.substr(1)
                   << " seq_len=" << model.seq_len
                   << " d_model=" << model.d_model
                   << " nhead=" << model.nhead
                   << " layers=" << model.num_layers
                   << " output_dim=" << model.output_dim
                   << " transposed_linear_weights=1"
                   << "\n";
 
         for (std::int64_t batch : args.batches) {
             torch::Tensor states = beam::stream1_libtorch::make_states(batch, model.state_len, model.num_classes, device);
             double elapsed_ms = 0.0;
             torch::Tensor logits = args.cuda_graph
                 ? run_cuda_graph(model, states, args.warmup, args.iters, device, elapsed_ms)
                 : run_eager(model, states, args.warmup, args.iters, device, elapsed_ms);
             const double parents_per_sec = (static_cast<double>(batch) * args.iters) / (elapsed_ms / 1000.0);
             const double candidates_per_sec = parents_per_sec * model.move_count;
             const torch::Tensor keys = beam::stream1_libtorch::score_keys(logits);
             const auto checksum = keys.sum().item<std::int64_t>();
+            const std::uint64_t score_digest = score_key_digest(keys);
             const std::string first_score_keys = format_first_score_keys(keys);
             std::cout << "stream1_transformer_libtorch_micro"
                       << " mode=" << mode

                       << " elapsed_ms=" << elapsed_ms

             if (csv) {

 namespace beam::bench {
 
+constexpr std::uint64_t FNV64_OFFSET = 1469598103934665603ULL;
+constexpr std::uint64_t FNV64_PRIME = 1099511628211ULL;
+
 struct ScoreSummary {
     std::uint64_t checksum = 0;
+    std::uint64_t score_key_digest = FNV64_OFFSET;
     std::string first_score_keys;
 };
 
 ScoreSummary summarize_score_buffers(
     const std::vector<std::uint32_t*>& score_buffers,
     std::uint32_t b_micro,
     std::uint32_t concurrent) {
     ScoreSummary summary;
     std::vector<std::uint32_t> host(static_cast<std::uint64_t>(b_micro) * MOVE_COUNT);
     for (std::uint32_t lane = 0; lane < concurrent; ++lane) {
         BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(
             host.data(),
             score_buffers[lane],
             host.size() * sizeof(std::uint32_t),
             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
         for (std::uint32_t value : host) {
             summary.checksum += value;
+            for (int shift = 0; shift < 32; shift += 8) {
+                summary.score_key_digest ^= static_cast<std::uint64_t>((value >> shift) & 0xFFU);
+                summary.score_key_digest *= FNV64_PRIME;
+            }
         }
         if (lane == 0 && !host.empty()) {
             std::ostringstream out;
             for (std::uint32_t move = 0; move < MOVE_COUNT; ++move) {
                 if (move != 0) {
                     out << ',';
                 }
                 out << host[move];
             }
             summary.first_score_keys = out.str();
         }
     }
     return summary;
 }
````

```text
124:
125:## User preferences
126:
127:- when launching on MEPhI HPC, the user said all jobs should use one precompiled beam-search binary -> default to a shared prebuilt `production_runner` instead of rebuilding per puzzle task [Task 1]
128:- when clarifying array behavior, the user made `PUZZLE_LIMIT=1` mean one puzzle per array task, not one puzzle total -> keep the array-wide coverage explicit when preparing launch scripts [Task 1]
129:- when shaping the GitHub output, the user asked to publish "right after each solved puzzle" and keep each puzzle in its own clearly named folder -> prefer per-puzzle publish-on-solve structure like `p0027` over monolithic dumps [Task 2]
130:- when there were too many cluster files, the user asked to write the inventory into a file on GitHub and read from there -> future aggregation should be file-driven and low-noise rather than terminal-stream-driven [Task 2][Task 3]
131:
132:## Reusable knowledge
133:
134:- `hpc/ihes_cube_model/prepare_ihes_prebuilt_runner.sh` builds the shared binary at `/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100/ihes_cube_model/prebuilt-a100-ihes/production_runner`, and `hpc/ihes_cube_model/ihes_solve_bucket_from_scratch.sh` already knows how to consume `BEAM_PREBUILT_RUNNER` [Task 1]
135:- the verified runtime on basis exposed `world_size_effective=8`, `NCCL 2.21.5+cuda12.4`, and eight visible A100-40GB GPUs, so the prebuilt-runner path was validated on the actual target hardware [Task 1]
136:- the durable results layout is `data/ihes_cube/puzzles/pXXXX/solutions.tsv`, `unique_solutions.tsv`, `metadata.env`, `summary.md`, plus root-level `index.tsv` and `improvements.tsv`; non-full-solution repair artifacts belong under separate `repair_segments/` trees [Task 2]
137:- the published cluster inventory lives under `data/ihes_cube/raw_inventory/` and should be the first routing layer when a future agent needs to inspect the cluster-side file set without reopening the live cluster [Task 2][Task 3]
138:- full bucket results should be deduplicated by `(variant, source_index, found_length, solution_path, source_solution)`, while resident repair segments and legacy repair segments should be kept in their own schemas rather than merged into full-solution tables [Task 3]
139:
140:## Failures and how to do differently
141:
142:- symptom: each array task recompiles instead of reusing the solver. cause: `BEAM_PREBUILT_RUNNER` was not wired through a separate prebuild dependency. fix: submit the prebuild first and make the puzzle array depend on it via `--dependency=afterok:<prebuild_job>` [Task 1]
143:- symptom: cluster-side publish fails after compute succeeds. cause: the compute node cannot resolve `github.com` or otherwise lacks GitHub network access. fix: tolerate cluster-side push failure and leave local commits/results ready for later push from a node with working DNS/network [Task 2]
144:- symptom: the backfill/index writer crashes on old rows. cause: metadata rows carried extra keys such as `variants` and `source_files` that were not declared in the index schema. fix: widen the declared schema and filter outgoing rows to declared fields before writing [Task 2][Task 3]
145:- symptom: one-pass "aggregate everything" scripts become noisy and brittle. cause: mixed artifact families and header-only files are treated uniformly. fix: partition by artifact type first and skip empty/header-only files (`st_size <= 200` or similar threshold) [Task 3]
```

Time: `2026-07-05T09:05:55.280Z` · source line `170`

```text
**Findings**
- Real digest parity has not been executed yet. The recorded post-`99810b6` artifact is dry-run only, and notes say no GPU parity run was performed because weights/binaries were not present: [test_results/stream1_transformer_score_digest_parity_2026-07-05.md](D:/100XH100/.worktrees/stream1-piece-transformer/test_results/stream1_transformer_score_digest_parity_2026-07-05.md:67), [test_results/stream1_transformer_backend_parity_contract_2026-07-05.md](D:/100XH100/.worktrees/stream1-piece-transformer/test_results/stream1_transformer_backend_parity_contract_2026-07-05.md:69).
- Parity currently hard-codes all selected backends to `eager`, so it does not cover `libtorch/cuda_graph` or `native_cutlass/graph`, despite those being registered modes: [tools/stream1_transformer_parity.py](D:/100XH100/.worktrees/stream1-piece-transformer/tools/stream1_transformer_parity.py:260), [tools/stream1_transformer_backends.py](D:/100XH100/.worktrees/stream1-piece-transformer/tools/stream1_transformer_backends.py:64).
- Native real execution also depends on CUTLASS FMHA example headers. CMake sets `BEAM_HAS_CUTLASS_FMHA=0` if they are missing, and fp16 attention then throws instead of running: [CMakeLists.txt](D:/100XH100/.worktrees/stream1-piece-transformer/CMakeLists.txt:128), [cuda/stream1_transformer.cu](D:/100XH100/.worktrees/stream1-piece-transformer/cuda/stream1_transformer.cu:663).

**Speed Read**
- The speed gap is not from missing tensor cores. The profile says both cuBLASLt and CUTLASS use HMMA; native is limited by GEMM epilogue/layout/memory behavior and LayerNorm/global-memory traffic: [test_results/stream1_transformer_cutlass_vs_libtorch_profile_2026-07-04.md](D:/100XH100/.worktrees/stream1-piece-transformer/test_results/stream1_transformer_cutlass_vs_libtorch_profile_2026-07-04.md:77).
- Native still has separate residual GEMM then bias-add kernels per transformer block, which is exactly the kind of extra global-memory pass the profile points at: [cuda/stream1_transformer.cu](D:/100XH100/.worktrees/stream1-piece-transformer/cuda/stream1_transformer.cu:1325), [cuda/stream1_transformer.cu](D:/100XH100/.worktrees/stream1-piece-transformer/cuda/stream1_transformer.cu:1334).
- LibTorch also materializes a fairly allocation-heavy token build with full gather/mask/add per slot, while the PyTorch reference masks first and adds in-place: [tools/stream1_transformer_libtorch_backend.hpp](D:/100XH100/.worktrees/stream1-piece-transformer/tools/stream1_transformer_libtorch_backend.hpp:276), [tools/stream1_transformer_torch_benchmark.py](D:/100XH100/.worktrees/stream1-piece-transformer/tools/stream1_transformer_torch_benchmark.py:193).

**Next Changes**
1. Extend the parity tool to accept backend modes, at least `pytorch:eager,libtorch:eager,libtorch:cuda_graph,native_cutlass:eager,native_cutlass:graph`, then record one real digest run. This is the safest correctness improvement.
2. Add a native-only CUTLASS residual+bias epilogue for attention-out and FF2, replacing the separate `stream1_transformer_bias_add_launch` calls after residual GEMMs. Keep the current path behind the existing function until digest parity passes. MLP stays untouched.


```
